<script>
// ===== KaTeX: re-render visible math after dynamic UI changes =====
window.rerenderMath = function(root) {
  if (typeof renderMathInElement !== 'function') return;
  var el = root || document.body;
  if (!el || !el.querySelector) return;
  try {
    renderMathInElement(el, {
      delimiters: [
        { left: '$$', right: '$$', display: true },
        { left: '$', right: '$', display: false },
        { left: '\\(', right: '\\)', display: false }
      ],
      ignoredClasses: ['katex', 'katex-display', 'katex-mathml']
    });
  } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
};

// ===== Theme toggle =====
function toggleTheme() {
  var html = document.documentElement;
  var btn = document.getElementById('themeBtn');
  if (html.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark') {
    html.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');
    btn.textContent = '\u{1F319} Dark';
  } else {
    html.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
    btn.textContent = '\u{2600} Light';
  }
  if (typeof window.updatePresBarChartTheme === 'function') window.updatePresBarChartTheme();
  var modeRoot = document.body.getAttribute('data-mode') === 'present'
    ? document.getElementById('present-mode')
    : document.getElementById('study-mode');
  if (modeRoot && typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') {
    setTimeout(function() { window.rerenderMath(modeRoot); }, 0);
  }
}

// ===== Professor View toggle =====
function toggleProfView() {
  document.body.classList.toggle('prof-view');
  var btn = document.getElementById('profBtn');
  btn.classList.toggle('active');
  btn.textContent = document.body.classList.contains('prof-view') ? 'Professor View \u2713' : 'Professor View';
}


// ===== IntersectionObserver for sidebar =====
var sections = document.querySelectorAll('#study-mode section');
var navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar a');
var observer = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
  entries.forEach(function(entry) {
    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
      navLinks.forEach(function(l) { l.classList.remove('active'); });
      var link = document.querySelector('.sidebar a[href="#' + entry.target.id + '"]');
      if (link) { link.classList.add('active'); link.classList.add('visited'); }
    }
  });
}, { rootMargin: '-20% 0px -60% 0px' });
sections.forEach(function(s) { observer.observe(s); });

// ===== G4: reset FiLM demo sliders when leaving §04 (viewport) =====
function resetFilmSliders() {
  var cg = document.getElementById('channelGamma');
  var cb = document.getElementById('channelBeta');
  var gs = document.getElementById('gammaSlider');
  var bs = document.getElementById('betaSlider');
  if (cg) cg.value = '100';
  if (cb) cb.value = '0';
  if (gs) gs.value = '100';
  if (bs) bs.value = '0';
  if (typeof updateChannelGrid === 'function') updateChannelGrid();
  if (typeof updateGammaVis === 'function') updateGammaVis();
}
(function() {
  var filmEl = document.getElementById('film');
  if (!filmEl) return;
  var filmLeave = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
    entries.forEach(function(entry) {
      if (!entry.isIntersecting) resetFilmSliders();
    });
  }, { threshold: 0, rootMargin: '0px' });
  filmLeave.observe(filmEl);
})();

// ===== Copy code =====
function copyCode(btn) {
  var code = btn.parentElement.querySelector('code');
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(code.textContent);
  btn.textContent = 'Copied!';
  setTimeout(function() { btn.textContent = 'Copy'; }, 1500);
}

// ===== Accordion toggle =====
function toggleAccordion(btn) {
  var body = btn.nextElementSibling;
  var arrow = btn.querySelector('.accordion-arrow');
  var isOpen = body.style.maxHeight && body.style.maxHeight !== '0px';
  if (isOpen) {
    body.style.maxHeight = '0px';
    body.style.padding = '0 20px';
    arrow.textContent = '\u25B8';
  } else {
    body.style.maxHeight = body.scrollHeight + 40 + 'px';
    body.style.padding = '16px 20px';
    arrow.textContent = '\u25BE';
    if (typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') {
      setTimeout(function() { window.rerenderMath(body); }, 0);
    }
  }
}

// ===== Gamma/Beta feature visualization =====
var origValues = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.7, 0.1, 0.8, 0.4, 0.2, 0.6, 0.4, 0.3, 0.9, 0.7, 0.1, 0.5, 0.3];

function createGrid(container, values) {
  container.innerHTML = '';
  values.forEach(function(v) {
    var cell = document.createElement('div');
    cell.className = 'feat-cell';
    var clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
    var r = Math.round(56 + clamped * 150);
    var g = Math.round(189 + clamped * 40);
    var b = 248;
    cell.style.background = 'rgba(' + r + ',' + g + ',' + b + ',' + (0.1 + clamped * 0.9) + ')';
    container.appendChild(cell);
  });
}

function updateGammaVis() {
  var gammaSlider = document.getElementById('gammaSlider');
  var gammaVal = document.getElementById('gammaVal');
  if (!gammaSlider || !gammaVal) return;
  var gamma = parseFloat(gammaSlider.value) / 100;
  gammaVal.textContent = gamma.toFixed(2);
  var origFeat = document.getElementById('origFeat');
  var gammaFeat = document.getElementById('gammaFeat');
  if (origFeat) createGrid(origFeat, origValues);
  if (gammaFeat) createGrid(gammaFeat, origValues.map(function(v) { return v * gamma; }));
  var betaOrigFeat = document.getElementById('betaOrigFeat');
  if (betaOrigFeat) createGrid(betaOrigFeat, origValues.map(function(v) { return v * gamma; }));
  updateBetaVis();
}

function updateBetaVis() {
  var gammaSlider = document.getElementById('gammaSlider');
  var betaSlider = document.getElementById('betaSlider');
  var betaVal = document.getElementById('betaVal');
  if (!gammaSlider || !betaSlider || !betaVal) return;
  var gamma = parseFloat(gammaSlider.value) / 100;
  var beta = parseFloat(betaSlider.value) / 100;
  betaVal.textContent = beta.toFixed(2);
  var betaOrigFeat = document.getElementById('betaOrigFeat');
  var betaFeat = document.getElementById('betaFeat');
  if (betaOrigFeat) createGrid(betaOrigFeat, origValues.map(function(v) { return v * gamma; }));
  if (betaFeat) createGrid(betaFeat, origValues.map(function(v) { return v * gamma + beta; }));
}

// ===== Channel Grid Visualization (D1) — 16×16, deterministic base regions =====
function channelGridMulberry32(a) {
  return function() {
    var t = (a += 0x6d2b79f5);
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

function ensureChannelGridCells() {
  var grid = document.getElementById('channelGrid');
  if (!grid) return [];
  var existing = grid.querySelectorAll('.channel-box');
  if (existing.length === 256) return Array.prototype.slice.call(existing);
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  var cells = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
    var row = Math.floor(i / 16);
    var col = i % 16;
    var base = 0.6;
    /* Background (suppressed) */
    if (i <= 30) base = 0.10 + ((i % 7) * 0.03);
    /* Floor (suppressed) */
    else if (i <= 60) base = 0.15 + ((i % 6) * 0.03);
    /* Arm/wrist semantic groups (amplified) */
    else if ((i >= 180 && i <= 210) || (i >= 230 && i <= 255)) base = 0.85 + (((i * 13) % 100) / 100) * 0.9;
    /* Mid channels */
    else base = 0.40 + (((row + col) % 8) / 8) * 0.70;

    var cell = document.createElement('div');
    cell.className = 'channel-box channel-cell';
    cell.dataset.baseGamma = base.toFixed(4);
    cell.title = 'group ' + i + ' · base γ=' + base.toFixed(2);
    grid.appendChild(cell);
    cells.push(cell);
  }
  return cells;
}

function updateChannelGrid() {
  var gammaS = document.getElementById('channelGamma') || document.getElementById('channelGammaSlider');
  var betaS = document.getElementById('channelBeta') || document.getElementById('channelBetaSlider');
  if (!gammaS || !betaS) return;
  var gammaCtl = document.getElementById('gamma-slider');
  var betaCtl = document.getElementById('beta-slider');
  if (gammaCtl && gammaCtl.value !== gammaS.value) gammaCtl.value = gammaS.value;
  if (betaCtl && betaCtl.value !== betaS.value) betaCtl.value = betaS.value;
  var g = parseFloat(gammaS.value) / 100;
  var b = parseFloat(betaS.value) / 100;
  var gv = document.getElementById('channelGammaVal');
  var bv = document.getElementById('channelBetaVal');
  if (gv) gv.textContent = g.toFixed(2);
  if (bv) bv.textContent = b.toFixed(2);
  var gvCtl = document.getElementById('gamma-val');
  var bvCtl = document.getElementById('beta-val');
  if (gvCtl) gvCtl.textContent = g.toFixed(2);
  if (bvCtl) bvCtl.textContent = b.toFixed(2);

  var betaBar = document.getElementById('channelBetaBar');
  var betaLbl = document.getElementById('channelBetaBarLabel');
  if (betaBar) {
    var bw = Math.min(50, Math.abs(b) * 50);
    betaBar.style.width = bw + '%';
    betaBar.style.left = b >= 0 ? '50%' : (50 - bw) + '%';
  }
  if (betaLbl) betaLbl.textContent = 'Aggregate shift visualization: β = ' + (b >= 0 ? '+' : '') + b.toFixed(2) + ' (bar length ∝ |β|)';

  var cells = ensureChannelGridCells();
  cells.forEach(function(cell, idx) {
    var base = parseFloat(cell.dataset.baseGamma || '0.6');
    var eff = Math.max(0, Math.min(2.5, base * g + b));
    if (eff > 1.3) {
      cell.style.background = 'rgba(56,189,248,' + ((eff - 1) * 0.5).toFixed(3) + ')';
      cell.style.boxShadow = '0 0 4px rgba(56,189,248,0.6)';
      cell.style.borderColor = 'rgba(56,189,248,0.85)';
    } else if (eff < 0.4) {
      cell.style.background = 'rgba(10,15,30,0.90)';
      cell.style.boxShadow = 'none';
      cell.style.borderColor = 'rgba(71,85,105,0.4)';
    } else {
      cell.style.background = 'rgba(136,146,164,' + (eff * 0.3).toFixed(3) + ')';
      cell.style.boxShadow = 'none';
      cell.style.borderColor = 'rgba(100,116,139,0.45)';
    }
    cell.title = 'group ' + idx + ' · base γ=' + base.toFixed(2) + ' · effective=' + eff.toFixed(3);
  });
}

function bindFilmController() {
  var gammaCtl = document.getElementById('gamma-slider');
  var betaCtl = document.getElementById('beta-slider');
  var gammaS = document.getElementById('channelGamma') || document.getElementById('channelGammaSlider');
  var betaS = document.getElementById('channelBeta') || document.getElementById('channelBetaSlider');
  if (!gammaCtl || !betaCtl || !gammaS || !betaS) return;
  gammaCtl.addEventListener('input', function() {
    gammaS.value = gammaCtl.value;
    updateChannelGrid();
  });
  betaCtl.addEventListener('input', function() {
    betaS.value = betaCtl.value;
    updateChannelGrid();
  });
}

function initCmHeat16() {
  var el = document.getElementById('cmHeat16');
  if (!el || el.dataset.inited === '1') return;
  el.dataset.inited = '1';
  var n = 16;
  var rand = channelGridMulberry32(0x636d3136);
  for (var r = 0; r < n; r++) {
    for (var c = 0; c < n; c++) {
      var cell = document.createElement('div');
      cell.className = 'cmh-cell';
      var v = rand() * 0.14;
      if (r === c) v = 0.52 + rand() * 0.38;
      if (Math.abs(r - c) === 1) v += 0.09;
      v = Math.min(1, v);
      cell.style.background = 'rgba(56,189,248,' + (0.1 + v * 0.82) + ')';
      cell.title = 'norm mass r=' + r + ', c=' + c + ' → ' + v.toFixed(2);
      el.appendChild(cell);
    }
  }
}

// ===== Activation Explorer =====
function sigmoid(z) { return 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-z)); }

function updateActivationExplorer() {
  var slider = document.getElementById('actZSlider');
  if (!slider) return;
  var z = parseInt(slider.value, 10) / 100;
  document.getElementById('actZVal').textContent = z.toFixed(2);
  var s = sigmoid(z);
  var t = Math.tanh(z);
  var g = 1 + t;
  var bs = document.getElementById('barSigma');
  var bt = document.getElementById('barTanh');
  var bg = document.getElementById('barGammaAct');
  if (bs) bs.style.width = Math.min(100, (s / 2) * 100) + '%';
  if (bt) bt.style.width = Math.min(100, ((t + 1) / 2) * 100) + '%';
  if (bg) bg.style.width = Math.min(100, (g / 2) * 100) + '%';
}

// ===== Wing Loss Explorer =====
function wingLWing(ax, w, eps) {
  var C = w - w * Math.log(1 + w / eps);
  return ax < w ? w * Math.log(1 + ax / eps) : ax - C;
}

function wingGrad(ax, w, eps) {
  return ax < w ? w / (eps + ax) : 1;
}

function updateWingExplorer() {
  var axSlider = document.getElementById('wingAbsX');
  if (!axSlider) return;
  var ax = parseInt(axSlider.value, 10) / 2000;
  var w = parseInt(document.getElementById('wingW').value, 10) / 1000;
  var eps = parseInt(document.getElementById('wingEps').value, 10) / 1000;
  document.getElementById('wingAbsXVal').textContent = ax.toFixed(4);
  document.getElementById('wingWVal').textContent = w.toFixed(3);
  document.getElementById('wingEpsVal').textContent = eps.toFixed(4);
  var Lw = wingLWing(ax, w, eps);
  var gw = wingGrad(ax, w, eps);
  var L2 = ax * ax;
  var L1 = ax;
  var g2 = 2 * ax;
  var g1 = 1;
  document.getElementById('wingLval').textContent = Lw.toFixed(5);
  document.getElementById('wingGval').textContent = gw.toFixed(4);
  document.getElementById('wingL2val').textContent = L2.toFixed(6);
  document.getElementById('wingL1val').textContent = L1.toFixed(4);
  var mg = Math.max(g2, g1, gw, 1e-9);
  document.getElementById('barGradL2').style.width = (g2 / mg * 100) + '%';
  document.getElementById('barGradL1').style.width = (g1 / mg * 100) + '%';
  document.getElementById('barGradWing').style.width = (gw / mg * 100) + '%';
}

// ===== CB-Focal Explorer =====
function updateFocalExplorer() {
  var ncSlider = document.getElementById('focalNc');
  if (!ncSlider) return;
  var nc = Math.max(1, parseInt(ncSlider.value, 10) || 1);
  var py = parseInt(document.getElementById('focalPy').value, 10) / 1000;
  var gF = parseInt(document.getElementById('focalGamma').value, 10) / 100;
  document.getElementById('focalNcVal').textContent = String(nc);
  document.getElementById('focalPyVal').textContent = py.toFixed(3);
  document.getElementById('focalGammaVal').textContent = gF.toFixed(2);
  var betaCB = 0.9999;
  var alpha = (1 - betaCB) / (1 - Math.pow(betaCB, nc));
  var mod = Math.pow(Math.max(1e-6, 1 - py), gF);
  var logp = -Math.log(Math.max(1e-8, py));
  var loss = alpha * mod * logp;
  document.getElementById('focalAlpha').textContent = alpha.toFixed(5);
  document.getElementById('focalMod').textContent = mod.toFixed(5);
  document.getElementById('focalLog').textContent = logp.toFixed(4);
  document.getElementById('focalLoss').textContent = loss.toFixed(5);
  var amax = 0.05;
  document.getElementById('barAlpha').style.width = Math.min(100, (alpha / amax) * 100) + '%';
  document.getElementById('barFocalMod').style.width = (mod * 100) + '%';
  document.getElementById('barFocalLoss').style.width = Math.min(100, (loss / 0.15) * 100) + '%';
}

// ===== Kendall Explorer =====
function clamp(x, lo, hi) { return Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, x)); }

function updateKendallExplorer() {
  var sdSlider = document.getElementById('kSd');
  if (!sdSlider) return;
  var sd = parseInt(sdSlider.value, 10) / 100;
  var sp = parseInt(document.getElementById('kSp').value, 10) / 100;
  var sa = parseInt(document.getElementById('kSa').value, 10) / 100;
  var Ld = parseInt(document.getElementById('kLd').value, 10) / 1000;
  var Lp = parseInt(document.getElementById('kLp').value, 10) / 1000;
  var La = parseInt(document.getElementById('kLa').value, 10) / 1000;
  document.getElementById('kSdVal').textContent = sd.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('kSpVal').textContent = sp.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('kSaVal').textContent = sa.toFixed(2);
  document.getElementById('kLdVal').textContent = Ld.toFixed(3);
  document.getElementById('kLpVal').textContent = Lp.toFixed(3);
  document.getElementById('kLaVal').textContent = La.toFixed(3);
  var cd = clamp(sd, -4, 2);
  var cp = clamp(sp, -4, 2);
  var ca = clamp(sa, -4, 2);
  var wd = Math.exp(-cd);
  var wp = Math.exp(-cp);
  var wa = Math.exp(-ca);
  var td = wd * Ld + cd;
  var tp = wp * Lp + cp;
  var ta = wa * La + ca;
  var tot = td + tp + ta;
  document.getElementById('kTermD').textContent = td.toFixed(4);
  document.getElementById('kTermP').textContent = tp.toFixed(4);
  document.getElementById('kTermA').textContent = ta.toFixed(4);
  document.getElementById('kTotal').textContent = tot.toFixed(4);
  var ad = Math.abs(wd * Ld);
  var ap = Math.abs(wp * Lp);
  var aa = Math.abs(wa * La);
  var mx = Math.max(ad, ap, aa, 1e-9);
  document.getElementById('barKd').style.width = (ad / mx * 100) + '%';
  document.getElementById('barKp').style.width = (ap / mx * 100) + '%';
  document.getElementById('barKa').style.width = (aa / mx * 100) + '%';
}

// ===== Pyramid slider (C2–C5): bars + labels (alias for older markup) =====
function updatePyramidViz() { updatePyramidSlider(); }

function updatePyramidSlider() {
  var slider = document.getElementById('pyramidSlider');
  if (!slider) return;
  var level = parseInt(slider.value, 10);
  var shapes = [
    { short: 'C2', name: 'C2 (layer1)', shape: '[B, 256, 160, 120]', scale: '1/4', ch: 256, sp: 160 * 120 },
    { short: 'C3', name: 'C3 (layer2)', shape: '[B, 512, 80, 60]', scale: '1/8', ch: 512, sp: 80 * 60 },
    { short: 'C4', name: 'C4 (layer3)', shape: '[B, 1024, 40, 30]', scale: '1/16', ch: 1024, sp: 40 * 30 },
    { short: 'C5', name: 'C5 (layer4)', shape: '[B, 2048, 20, 15]', scale: '1/32', ch: 2048, sp: 20 * 15 }
  ];
  level = Math.max(0, Math.min(level, shapes.length - 1));
  if (String(slider.value) !== String(level)) slider.value = String(level);
  var s = shapes[level];
  var labelEl = document.getElementById('pyramidLabel');
  var shapeEl = document.getElementById('pyramidShape');
  var barsEl = document.getElementById('pyramidBars');
  if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = s.name + ' · ' + s.scale;
  if (shapeEl) shapeEl.textContent = s.shape;
  if (barsEl) {
    barsEl.innerHTML = '';
    var maxCh = 2048;
    var minSp = shapes[3].sp;
    var maxSp = shapes[0].sp;
    shapes.forEach(function(t, i) {
      var bar = document.createElement('div');
      bar.className = 'pyramid-bar';
      bar.title = t.name + ' ' + t.shape;
      var hCh = (t.ch / maxCh) * 100;
      var hSp = (t.sp / maxSp) * 100;
      var h = 0.35 * hCh + 0.65 * hSp;
      bar.style.height = Math.max(8, h) + '%';
      bar.style.opacity = i === level ? '1' : '0.35';
      bar.style.boxShadow = i === level ? '0 0 0 2px var(--teal)' : 'none';
      barsEl.appendChild(bar);
    });
  }
}

// ===== Heatmap canvas + soft-argmax (sliders hmX / hmY) =====
function drawHeatmapSoftmax() {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('hmCanvas');
  if (!canvas || !canvas.getContext) return;
  var sx = document.getElementById('hmX');
  var sy = document.getElementById('hmY');
  if (!sx || !sy) return;
  var peakX = parseFloat(sx.value);
  var peakY = parseFloat(sy.value);
  var vx = document.getElementById('hmXVal');
  var vy = document.getElementById('hmYVal');
  if (vx) vx.textContent = String(Math.round(peakX));
  if (vy) vy.textContent = String(Math.round(peakY));

  var gw = 60;
  var gh = 40;
  var sigma = 4.5;
  var logits = [];
  var i, j, idx, dx, dy, v;
  for (j = 0; j < gh; j++) {
    for (i = 0; i < gw; i++) {
      dx = i - peakX;
      dy = j - peakY;
      v = -(dx * dx + dy * dy) / (2 * sigma * sigma);
      logits.push(v);
    }
  }
  var maxL = logits[0];
  for (idx = 1; idx < logits.length; idx++) {
    if (logits[idx] > maxL) maxL = logits[idx];
  }
  var expSum = 0;
  var probs = new Array(logits.length);
  for (idx = 0; idx < logits.length; idx++) {
    probs[idx] = Math.exp(logits[idx] - maxL);
    expSum += probs[idx];
  }
  for (idx = 0; idx < probs.length; idx++) {
    probs[idx] /= expSum;
  }
  var ex = 0;
  var ey = 0;
  idx = 0;
  for (j = 0; j < gh; j++) {
    for (i = 0; i < gw; i++) {
      ex += probs[idx] * i;
      ey += probs[idx] * j;
      idx++;
    }
  }

  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  var cw = canvas.width;
  var ch = canvas.height;
  var cellW = cw / gw;
  var cellH = ch / gh;
  var img = ctx.createImageData(cw, ch);
  var p, px, py, r, g, b, a, base;
  for (py = 0; py < ch; py++) {
    for (px = 0; px < cw; px++) {
      i = Math.min(gw - 1, Math.floor(px / cellW));
      j = Math.min(gh - 1, Math.floor(py / cellH));
      p = probs[j * gw + i];
      r = Math.round(15 + p * 220);
      g = Math.round(40 + p * 180);
      b = Math.round(80 + p * 200);
      a = 255;
      base = (py * cw + px) * 4;
      img.data[base] = r;
      img.data[base + 1] = g;
      img.data[base + 2] = b;
      img.data[base + 3] = a;
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#fbbf24';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
  ctx.setLineDash([4, 3]);
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(ex * cellW + cellW * 0.5, ey * cellH + cellH * 0.5, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  var out = document.getElementById('hmSoftArgmax');
  if (out) {
    out.textContent = 'soft-argmax (x, y) = (' + ex.toFixed(2) + ', ' + ey.toFixed(2) + ')  —  matches Gaussian peak (' + peakX.toFixed(1) + ', ' + peakY.toFixed(1) + ')';
  }
}

/** Legacy name: old v5 draft used heatmapTemp + grid; keep no-op guard */
function updateHeatmapDemo() {
  if (document.getElementById('hmCanvas')) drawHeatmapSoftmax();
}

// ===== Receptive field demo =====
function updateReceptiveField() {
  var sel = document.getElementById('rfSelect');
  if (!sel) return;
  var level = sel.value;
  var data = {
    'C2': { rf: '43x43', stride: 4, spatial: '160x120' },
    'C3': { rf: '99x99', stride: 8, spatial: '80x60' },
    'C4': { rf: '227x227', stride: 16, spatial: '40x30' },
    'C5': { rf: '483x483', stride: 32, spatial: '20x15' }
  };
  var d = data[level];
  var rfEl = document.getElementById('rfSize');
  var strideEl = document.getElementById('rfStride');
  var spatialEl = document.getElementById('rfSpatial');
  if (rfEl) rfEl.textContent = d.rf;
  if (strideEl) strideEl.textContent = d.stride;
  if (spatialEl) spatialEl.textContent = d.spatial;
}

// ===== CB Calculator =====
function updateCBCalc() {
  var ncInput = document.getElementById('ncInput');
  var expl = document.getElementById('cbExplanation');
  var out = document.getElementById('alphaResult');
  if (!ncInput || !out) return;
  var raw = ncInput.value.trim();
  var nc = parseInt(raw, 10);
  if (!isFinite(nc) || raw === '' || nc < 1) {
    out.textContent = '—';
    if (expl) expl.textContent = nc <= 0 && raw !== '' ? 'Enter n_c ≥ 1 (class count must be positive).' : 'Enter a positive integer for n_c.';
    return;
  }
  var beta = 0.9999;
  var alpha = (1 - beta) / (1 - Math.pow(beta, nc));
  var effectiveN = (1 - Math.pow(beta, nc)) / (1 - beta);
  out.textContent = alpha.toFixed(5);
  if (expl) {
    expl.textContent = 'Cui et al. effective-number mass (1−β^{n_c})/(1−β) = ' + effectiveN.toFixed(2) +
      '. Inverse used in α_c = (1−β)/(1−β^{n_c}) = ' + alpha.toFixed(5) + '.';
  }
}

function destroyChartIfAny(canvas) {
  if (typeof Chart === 'undefined' || !canvas) return;
  try {
    var existing = Chart.getChart(canvas);
    if (existing) existing.destroy();
  } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
}

/** Activity §7a: staged CE → +CB → +Focal */
function showCbStage(n) {
  var b1 = document.getElementById('cbStageBlock1');
  var b2 = document.getElementById('cbStageBlock2');
  var b3 = document.getElementById('cbStageBlock3');
  if (!b2 || !b3) return;
  n = Math.max(1, Math.min(3, n | 0));
  if (b1) b1.style.display = 'block';
  b2.style.display = n >= 2 ? 'block' : 'none';
  b3.style.display = n >= 3 ? 'block' : 'none';
  var fp = document.getElementById('formal-problem-block');
  if (fp && typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') {
    setTimeout(function() { window.rerenderMath(fp); }, 0);
  }
}

/** Focal factor (1−p)^γ vs p for several γ (§7a chart) */
function initFocalGammaChart() {
  var ctx = document.getElementById('focalGammaChart');
  if (!ctx || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
  destroyChartIfAny(ctx);
  try {
    var ps = [];
    var i;
    for (i = 2; i <= 98; i += 2) {
      ps.push((i / 100).toFixed(2));
    }
    function series(gamma) {
      return ps.map(function(p) {
        var pv = parseFloat(p, 10);
        return Math.pow(1 - pv, gamma);
      });
    }
    new Chart(ctx, {
      type: 'line',
      data: {
        labels: ps,
        datasets: [
          { label: 'γ=0', data: series(0), borderColor: '#8892a4', borderWidth: 1.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false },
          { label: 'γ=1', data: series(1), borderColor: '#fb923c', borderWidth: 1.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false },
          { label: 'γ=2 (RetinaNet)', data: series(2), borderColor: '#38bdf8', borderWidth: 2.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false },
          { label: 'γ=5', data: series(5), borderColor: '#c084fc', borderWidth: 1.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false, borderDash: [4, 3] }
        ]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: {
          legend: { labels: { color: '#8892a4', font: { size: 11 } } },
          title: { display: true, text: '(1 − p_y)^γ  vs  model confidence p_y on true class', color: '#8892a4', font: { size: 12 } },
          subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Generic focal factor; POPW uses γ=2 on CB-Focal in the reported config', color: '#64748b', font: { size: 10 }, padding: { bottom: 4 } }
        },
        scales: {
          x: { title: { display: true, text: 'p_y', color: '#8892a4' }, ticks: { color: '#4a5568', maxTicksLimit: 12 }, grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' } },
          y: { title: { display: true, text: 'Focal factor', color: '#8892a4' }, min: 0, max: 1.02, ticks: { color: '#4a5568' }, grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' } }
        }
      }
    });
  } catch (e) { console.warn('initFocalGammaChart', e); }
}

// ===== Loss comparison chart =====
function initLossChart() {
  var ctx = document.getElementById('lossChart');
  if (!ctx || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
  destroyChartIfAny(ctx);
  try {
  var xs = [];
  var l2 = [], l1 = [], huber = [], wing = [];
  var w = 0.05, eps = 0.005, delta = 0.01;
  var C = w - w * Math.log(1 + w / eps);

  for (var i = -40; i <= 40; i++) {
    var x = i * 0.005;
    xs.push(x.toFixed(3));
    var ax = Math.abs(x);
    l2.push(x * x);
    l1.push(ax);
    huber.push(ax < delta ? 0.5 * x * x : delta * (ax - 0.5 * delta));
    wing.push(ax < w ? w * Math.log(1 + ax / eps) : ax - C);
  }

  window.popwLossChart = new Chart(ctx, {
    type: 'line',
    data: {
      labels: xs,
      datasets: [
        { label: 'L2 (MSE)', data: l2, borderColor: '#f87171', borderWidth: 1.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false, hidden: false },
        { label: 'L1 (MAE)', data: l1, borderColor: '#8892a4', borderWidth: 1.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false, hidden: false },
        { label: 'Huber (\u03b4=0.01)', data: huber, borderColor: '#fb923c', borderWidth: 1.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false, hidden: false },
        { label: 'Wing (w=0.05, \u03b5=0.005)', data: wing, borderColor: '#38bdf8', borderWidth: 2.5, pointRadius: 0, fill: false, hidden: false }
      ]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      plugins: {
        legend: {
          labels: { color: '#8892a4', font: { family: 'Inter', size: 11 } },
          onClick: function(e, legendItem, legend) {
            var i = legendItem.datasetIndex;
            var c = legend.chart;
            if (c.isDatasetVisible(i)) c.hide(i); else c.show(i);
            c.update();
          }
        },
        title: { display: true, text: 'Loss Function Comparison (normalized coordinate error) — click legend to toggle', color: '#8892a4', font: { family: 'Inter', size: 13 } },
        subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Wing curve uses training hyperparameters w=0.05, ε=0.005 (same as §6)', color: '#64748b', font: { family: 'Inter', size: 10 }, padding: { bottom: 4 } }
      },
      scales: {
        x: { title: { display: true, text: 'Error |x|', color: '#8892a4' }, ticks: { color: '#4a5568', maxTicksLimit: 10 }, grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' } },
        y: { title: { display: true, text: 'Loss', color: '#8892a4' }, ticks: { color: '#4a5568' }, grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' }, max: 0.15 }
      }
    }
  });
  } catch (e) { console.warn('initLossChart', e); }
}

// ===== Wing gradient comparison chart =====
function initWingGradientChart() {
  var ctx = document.getElementById('wingGradientChart');
  if (!ctx || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
  destroyChartIfAny(ctx);
  try {
    var w = 0.05, eps = 0.005, delta = 0.01;
    var xs = [];
    var si;
    for (si = -120; si <= 120; si++) xs.push(si * 0.001);
    function gradL2(x) { return 2 * x; }
    function gradL1(x) { return Math.abs(x) < 1e-14 ? 0 : Math.sign(x); }
    function gradHuber(x) {
      var ax = Math.abs(x);
      return ax <= delta ? x : delta * Math.sign(x);
    }
    function gradWingSigned(x) {
      var ax = Math.abs(x);
      if (ax < 1e-14) return 0;
      return Math.sign(x) * (ax < w ? w / (eps + ax) : 1);
    }
    var series = [
      { label: 'L2 gradient', fn: gradL2, bw: 2, c: '#ef4444' },
      { label: 'L1 gradient', fn: gradL1, bw: 2, c: '#f97316' },
      { label: 'Huber δ=0.01', fn: gradHuber, bw: 2, c: '#eab308' },
      { label: 'Wing gradient (ours)', fn: gradWingSigned, bw: 3, c: '#38bdf8' }
    ];
    var allY = [];
    series.forEach(function(s) {
      xs.forEach(function(x) { allY.push(Math.abs(s.fn(x))); });
    });
    var yMax = Math.max.apply(null, allY) * 1.08;
    if (!isFinite(yMax) || yMax < 0.01) yMax = 10;
    var yMin = -yMax;
    var datasets = series.map(function(s) {
      return {
        label: s.label,
        data: xs.map(function(x) { return { x: x, y: s.fn(x) }; }),
        borderColor: s.c,
        borderWidth: s.bw,
        pointRadius: 0,
        tension: 0.12,
        parsing: false
      };
    });
    datasets.push({
      label: '|x| = w (0.05)',
      data: [{ x: -w, y: yMin }, { x: -w, y: yMax }],
      borderColor: '#64748b',
      borderWidth: 1.5,
      borderDash: [5, 5],
      pointRadius: 0,
      parsing: false
    });
    datasets.push({
      label: '_vlineR',
      data: [{ x: w, y: yMin }, { x: w, y: yMax }],
      borderColor: '#64748b',
      borderWidth: 1.5,
      borderDash: [5, 5],
      pointRadius: 0,
      parsing: false
    });
    new Chart(ctx, {
      type: 'line',
      data: { datasets: datasets },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        interaction: { mode: 'nearest', intersect: false },
        plugins: {
          legend: {
            labels: {
              color: '#8892a4',
              font: { size: 11 },
              filter: function(item) { return item.text !== '_vlineR'; }
            }
          },
          title: {
            display: true,
            text: 'dL/dx vs normalized error (same w=0.05, ε=0.005 as losses.py wingGrad)',
            color: '#8892a4',
            font: { size: 12 }
          },
          subtitle: {
            display: true,
            text: 'Dashed verticals at |x|=w. Present deck §6 shows loss L(x); this Study chart is the gradient.',
            color: '#64748b',
            font: { size: 10 },
            padding: { bottom: 4 }
          }
        },
        scales: {
          x: {
            type: 'linear',
            title: { display: true, text: 'x (normalized coordinate error)', color: '#8892a4' },
            ticks: { color: '#4a5568' },
            grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' },
            min: -0.12,
            max: 0.12
          },
          y: {
            title: { display: true, text: 'dL/dx', color: '#8892a4' },
            ticks: { color: '#4a5568' },
            grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' },
            min: yMin,
            max: yMax
          }
        }
      }
    });
  } catch (e) { console.warn('initWingGradientChart', e); }
}

// ===== Class distribution chart =====
function initClassDistChart() {
  var ctx = document.getElementById('classDistChart');
  if (!ctx || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
  destroyChartIfAny(ctx);
  try {

  var IKEA_ACTIVITIES = [
    {name:'Lay (frame)', count:520},
    {name:'Attach (leg)', count:380},
    {name:'Flip', count:340},
    {name:'Lay (back)', count:310},
    {name:'Pick up', count:285},
    {name:'Tighten (screwdriver)', count:248},
    {name:'Attach (top)', count:215},
    {name:'Lay (front)', count:195},
    {name:'Tighten (hand)', count:178},
    {name:'Sit down', count:162},
    {name:'Stand up', count:148},
    {name:'Insert dowel', count:135},
    {name:'Hammer', count:98},
    {name:'Rotate', count:76},
    {name:'Spin (screwdriver)', count:58},
    {name:'NA', count:52}
  ].sort(function(a, b) { return b.count - a.count; });
  var labels = IKEA_ACTIVITIES.map(function(d){ return d.name; });
  var counts = IKEA_ACTIVITIES.map(function(d){ return d.count; });

  var colors = counts.map(function(c) {
    if (c > 500) return '#4a5568';
    if (c > 200) return '#fb923c';
    if (c > 100) return '#fbbf24';
    return '#f87171';
  });

  new Chart(ctx, {
    type: 'bar',
    data: {
      labels: labels,
      datasets: [{
        label: 'Approx. frame count',
        data: counts,
        backgroundColor: colors,
        borderWidth: 0,
        borderRadius: 2
      }]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      indexAxis: 'y',
      plugins: {
        legend: { display: false },
        title: {
          display: true,
          text: 'IKEA ASM activity counts (16-way summary, sorted by illustrative frequency)',
          color: '#8892a4',
          font: { family: 'Inter', size: 12 }
        },
        subtitle: {
          display: true,
          text: 'Not sourced from ikea_dataset.py or repo CSV — illustrative long-tail shape only; use training logs for real priors',
          color: '#64748b',
          font: { family: 'Inter', size: 10 },
          padding: { bottom: 4 }
        }
      },
      scales: {
        x: {
          title: { display: true, text: 'Frame count', color: '#8892a4' },
          ticks: { color: '#4a5568' },
          grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' }
        },
        y: {
          ticks: { color: '#8892a4', font: { family: 'JetBrains Mono', size: 9 } },
          grid: { display: false }
        }
      }
    }
  });
  } catch (e) { console.warn('initClassDistChart', e); }
}

// ===== CNN vs Transformer crossover chart =====
function initCrossoverChart() {
  var ctx = document.getElementById('crossoverChart');
  if (!ctx || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
  destroyChartIfAny(ctx);
  try {

  var sampleSizes = [1000, 2000, 5000, 10000, 50000, 100000, 500000, 1000000, 5000000, 10000000];
  var labels = sampleSizes.map(function(s) {
    if (s >= 1000000) return (s / 1000000) + 'M';
    if (s >= 1000) return (s / 1000) + 'K';
    return String(s);
  });

  var resnet = [62, 68, 74, 78, 82, 84, 85.5, 86, 86.2, 86.3];
  var vit = [35, 42, 52, 60, 72, 78, 85, 88, 91, 92.5];

  var chart = new Chart(ctx, {
    type: 'line',
    data: {
      labels: labels,
      datasets: [
        {
          label: 'ResNet-50 (illustrative)',
          data: resnet,
          borderColor: '#38bdf8',
          backgroundColor: 'rgba(56,189,248,0.1)',
          borderWidth: 2.5,
          pointRadius: 4,
          pointBackgroundColor: '#38bdf8',
          fill: false,
          tension: 0.3
        },
        {
          label: 'ViT-B/16 (illustrative)',
          data: vit,
          borderColor: '#a78bfa',
          backgroundColor: 'rgba(167,139,250,0.1)',
          borderWidth: 2.5,
          pointRadius: 4,
          pointBackgroundColor: '#a78bfa',
          fill: false,
          tension: 0.3
        }
      ]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      animation: {
        onComplete: function() {
          try {
            var c = Chart.getChart(ctx);
            if (!c || !c.scales.x || !c.scales.y) return;
            var xAxis = c.scales.x;
            var yAxis = c.scales.y;
            var ourN = 10000;
            var i0 = 2;
            var i1 = 3;
            var n0 = sampleSizes[i0];
            var n1 = sampleSizes[i1];
            var t = (ourN - n0) / (n1 - n0);
            t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
            var x0 = typeof xAxis.getPixelForValue === 'function' ? xAxis.getPixelForValue(i0) : xAxis.getPixelForTick(i0);
            var x1 = typeof xAxis.getPixelForValue === 'function' ? xAxis.getPixelForValue(i1) : xAxis.getPixelForTick(i1);
            if (typeof x0 !== 'number' || typeof x1 !== 'number' || !isFinite(x0) || !isFinite(x1)) return;
            var xPos = x0 + t * (x1 - x0);
            var g = c.ctx;
            g.save();
            g.strokeStyle = '#fbbf24';
            g.lineWidth = 2;
            g.setLineDash([6, 4]);
            g.beginPath();
            g.moveTo(xPos, yAxis.top);
            g.lineTo(xPos, yAxis.bottom);
            g.stroke();
            g.setLineDash([]);
            g.fillStyle = '#fbbf24';
            g.font = '11px system-ui, sans-serif';
            g.textAlign = 'left';
            g.fillText('Example low-data regime: 10K labels', xPos + 6, yAxis.top + 18);
            g.restore();
          } catch (err) { /* ignore draw errors */ }
        }
      },
      plugins: {
        legend: { labels: { color: '#8892a4', font: { family: 'Inter', size: 11 } } },
        title: {
          display: true,
          text: 'CNN vs Transformer: illustrative accuracy vs data size',
          color: '#8892a4',
          font: { family: 'Inter', size: 13 }
        },
        subtitle: {
          display: true,
          text: 'Synthetic crossover curve for pedagogy — not a POPW benchmark result',
          color: '#64748b',
          font: { family: 'Inter', size: 10 },
          padding: { bottom: 6 }
        }
      },
      scales: {
        x: {
          title: { display: true, text: 'Training samples (ordered)', color: '#8892a4' },
          ticks: { color: '#4a5568' },
          grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' }
        },
        y: {
          title: { display: true, text: 'Accuracy (%)', color: '#8892a4' },
          ticks: { color: '#4a5568' },
          grid: { color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)' },
          min: 30,
          max: 95
        }
      }
    }
  });
  chart.update();
  } catch (e) { console.warn('initCrossoverChart', e); }
}

// ===== Stepper (Worked Example with D4 term highlight) =====
var currentStep = 0;
var totalSteps = 11;

function showStep(n) {
  currentStep = n;
  document.querySelectorAll('#mainStepper .step-content').forEach(function(el, i) {
    el.classList.toggle('active', i === n);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#mainStepper .step-tab').forEach(function(el, i) {
    el.classList.toggle('active', i === n);
  });
  applyTermHighlight(n);
  var stepEl = document.getElementById('step' + n);
  if (stepEl && typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') {
    setTimeout(function() { window.rerenderMath(stepEl); }, 0);
  }
}

function nextStep() {
  if (currentStep < totalSteps - 1) showStep(currentStep + 1);
}

function prevStep() {
  if (currentStep > 0) showStep(currentStep - 1);
}

document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
  if (!e.key || (e.key !== 'ArrowLeft' && e.key !== 'ArrowRight')) return;
  var t = e.target;
  if (t && (t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.tagName === 'SELECT' || t.isContentEditable)) return;
  var stepper = document.getElementById('mainStepper');
  if (!stepper) return;
  var r = stepper.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (r.bottom < 40 || r.top > window.innerHeight - 40) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') nextStep();
  else prevStep();
});

function applyTermHighlight(stepIndex) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.term-highlight').forEach(function(el) {
    el.classList.remove('term-highlight');
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.step-keyterm').forEach(function(el) {
    el.classList.remove('term-active');
  });
  var activeStep = document.getElementById('step' + stepIndex);
  if (!activeStep) return;
  var focusEl = activeStep.querySelector('.step-keyterm');
  if (focusEl) focusEl.classList.add('term-active');
  var katexEls = activeStep.querySelectorAll('.katex');
  if (katexEls.length > 0) {
    katexEls[0].classList.add('term-highlight');
  }
}

// ===== Worst case gallery tabs =====
function showWorstCase(index) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.wc-content').forEach(function(el, i) {
    el.classList.toggle('active', i === index);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.wc-tab').forEach(function(el, i) {
    el.classList.toggle('active', i === index);
  });
}

function initLazyCharts() {
  var CHART_INIT = {
    lossChart: initLossChart,
    classDistChart: initClassDistChart,
    crossoverChart: initCrossoverChart,
    focalGammaChart: initFocalGammaChart,
    kendallSigmaChart: initKendallSigmaChart,
    wingGradientChart: initWingGradientChart
  };
  Object.keys(CHART_INIT).forEach(function(id) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.dataset.chart = id;
  });
  var observer = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
    entries.forEach(function(entry) {
      if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
      var canvas = entry.target;
      var key = canvas.dataset.chart;
      if (!key || canvas.dataset.chartInit === 'true') return;
      canvas.dataset.chartInit = 'true';
      var fn = CHART_INIT[key];
      if (typeof fn === 'function') fn();
      observer.unobserve(canvas);
    });
  }, { threshold: 0.1 });
  document.querySelectorAll('canvas[data-chart]').forEach(function(c) { observer.observe(c); });
}

function renderPoseVector() {
  var container = document.getElementById('pose-vector-viz');
  if (!container) return;
  var values = [
    0.00,0.12,-0.05,-0.03,0.14,-0.05,0.03,0.14,-0.05,-0.07,0.12,-0.04,0.07,0.12,-0.04,
    -0.18,0.35,0.08,0.18,0.35,0.10,-0.22,0.52,0.18,0.26,0.55,0.25,-0.23,0.71,0.38,0.28,0.71,0.42,
    0.00,0.00,0.00,0.06,0.00,0.02,-0.08,0.52,0.05,0.10,0.52,0.06,-0.09,0.96,0.04,0.11,0.96,0.05
  ];
  container.innerHTML = values.map(function(v, i) {
    var height = Math.max(5, Math.abs(v) * 100);
    var alpha = Math.max(0.3, Math.abs(v) * 2);
    var color = v >= 0 ? 'rgba(56,189,248,'+alpha+')' : 'rgba(251,146,60,'+alpha+')';
    var isWrist = i >= 27 && i <= 32;
    return '<div title="p['+i+']='+v.toFixed(3)+'" style="flex:1;height:100%;display:flex;align-items:flex-end;border:'+ (isWrist ? '1px solid #4ade80' : 'none') +';border-radius:1px;">' +
      '<div style="width:100%;height:'+height+'%;background:'+color+';border-radius:1px 1px 0 0;"></div></div>';
  }).join('');
}

function initRootNormalizationDemo() {
  var next = document.getElementById('rootNormNext');
  var title = document.getElementById('rootNormTitle');
  if (!next || !title) return;
  var idx = 0;
  var frames = [
    { id:'rootNormFrame1', title:'Raw pixel coordinates' },
    { id:'rootNormFrame2', title:'Hip midpoint computed' },
    { id:'rootNormFrame3', title:'Root-relative coordinates' }
  ];
  function paint() {
    frames.forEach(function(f, i) {
      var el = document.getElementById(f.id);
      if (el) el.style.display = i === idx ? 'block' : 'none';
    });
    title.textContent = frames[idx].title;
  }
  next.addEventListener('click', function() {
    idx = (idx + 1) % frames.length;
    paint();
  });
  paint();
}

function initFilmMiniGrids() {
  var concat = document.getElementById('concat-grid');
  var film = document.getElementById('film-grid');
  if (!concat || !film) return;
  var tc = getThemeColors();
  var tealRgb = hexToRgb(tc.teal);
  var textRgb = hexToRgb(tc.text);
  var bgRgb = hexToRgb(tc.bg);
  var channels = [];
  var i;
  for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) channels.push(0.15 + ((i * 37) % 100) / 100 * 0.8);
  function render(gamma) {
    concat.innerHTML = '';
    film.innerHTML = '';
    channels.forEach(function(v) {
      var c1 = document.createElement('div');
      c1.style.height = '14px';
      c1.style.background = 'rgba('+textRgb.r+','+textRgb.g+','+textRgb.b+',0.26)';
      c1.style.borderRadius = '2px';
      concat.appendChild(c1);
      var scaled = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v * gamma));
      var c2 = document.createElement('div');
      c2.style.height = '14px';
      c2.style.background = scaled > 0.5
        ? 'rgba('+tealRgb.r+','+tealRgb.g+','+tealRgb.b+',' + (0.25 + scaled * 0.7) + ')'
        : 'rgba('+bgRgb.r+','+bgRgb.g+','+bgRgb.b+',' + (0.2 + (0.5 - scaled) * 0.9) + ')';
      c2.style.borderRadius = '2px';
      film.appendChild(c2);
    });
  }
  render(1);
  document.querySelectorAll('.lf-gamma-slider').forEach(function(sl) {
    sl.addEventListener('input', function() { render(parseFloat(sl.value || '1')); });
  });
}

function initKendallScaleBars() {
  var wrap = document.getElementById('kendallScaleBars');
  if (!wrap) return;
  var vals = { wing: 8.3, bce: 0.4, focal: 0.8 };
  var max = vals.wing;
  var armed = false;
  function animate() {
    var wb = document.getElementById('scaleWingBar');
    var bb = document.getElementById('scaleBceBar');
    var fb = document.getElementById('scaleFocalBar');
    if (!wb || !bb || !fb) return;
    wb.style.width = (vals.wing / max * 100) + '%';
    bb.style.width = (vals.bce / max * 100) + '%';
    fb.style.width = (vals.focal / max * 100) + '%';
  }
  var io = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
    entries.forEach(function(e) {
      if (!e.isIntersecting || armed) return;
      armed = true;
      setTimeout(animate, 120);
      io.unobserve(wrap);
    });
  }, { threshold: 0.35 });
  io.observe(wrap);
}

function initNoveltyMatrix() {
  var svg = document.getElementById('noveltyMatrixSvg');
  if (!svg) return;
  var PRIOR_WORK = [
    {name:'ResNet-50 Baseline', year:'2016', xIdx:0, yIdx:0, color:'#6b7280'},
    {name:'OpenPose', year:'2017', xIdx:0, yIdx:0, color:'#6b7280'},
    {name:'HMR (Kanazawa)', year:'2018', xIdx:0, yIdx:0, color:'#6b7280'},
    {name:'Simple Baseline', year:'2018', xIdx:0, yIdx:0, color:'#6b7280'},
    {name:'Pose Concat', year:'2020', xIdx:1, yIdx:1, color:'#f97316'},
    {name:'POPW (no FiLM)', year:'ours', xIdx:1, yIdx:2, color:'#f97316'},
    {name:'POPW-FiLM (ours)', year:'ours', xIdx:2, yIdx:2, color:'#38bdf8', isOurs:true}
  ];
  var NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  while (svg.firstChild) svg.removeChild(svg.firstChild);
  var ox = 90, oy = 40, cw = 160, ch = 110;
  for (var r = 0; r < 3; r++) {
    for (var c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
      var rect = document.createElementNS(NS, 'rect');
      rect.setAttribute('x', ox + c*cw);
      rect.setAttribute('y', oy + r*ch);
      rect.setAttribute('width', cw - 4);
      rect.setAttribute('height', ch - 4);
      var fill = (c === 2 && r === 2) ? 'rgba(56,189,248,0.16)' : ((c === 2 || r === 2) ? 'rgba(148,163,184,0.08)' : 'rgba(15,23,42,0.35)');
      rect.setAttribute('fill', fill);
      rect.setAttribute('stroke', c === 2 && r === 2 ? '#38bdf8' : 'rgba(148,163,184,0.25)');
      rect.setAttribute('rx', '6');
      svg.appendChild(rect);
    }
  }
  ['No Pose Fusion','Concatenation','FiLM Conditioning (ours)'].forEach(function(t, i) {
    var tx = document.createElementNS(NS, 'text');
    tx.setAttribute('x', ox + i*cw + (cw-4)/2);
    tx.setAttribute('y', 22);
    tx.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
    tx.setAttribute('fill', i === 2 ? '#38bdf8' : '#94a3b8');
    tx.setAttribute('font-size', '11');
    tx.textContent = t;
    svg.appendChild(tx);
  });
  ['Standard Loss','Partial Multi-Task','Kendall Uncertainty'].forEach(function(t, i) {
    var tx = document.createElementNS(NS, 'text');
    tx.setAttribute('x', 12);
    tx.setAttribute('y', oy + i*ch + 56);
    tx.setAttribute('fill', i === 2 ? '#38bdf8' : '#94a3b8');
    tx.setAttribute('font-size', '11');
    tx.textContent = t;
    svg.appendChild(tx);
  });
  PRIOR_WORK.forEach(function(m, idx) {
    var cx = ox + m.xIdx*cw + 25 + ((idx*31) % 90);
    var cy = oy + m.yIdx*ch + 28 + ((idx*17) % 58);
    var dot = document.createElementNS(NS, 'circle');
    dot.setAttribute('cx', cx); dot.setAttribute('cy', cy); dot.setAttribute('r', m.isOurs ? 8 : 6);
    dot.setAttribute('fill', m.color);
    dot.setAttribute('stroke', m.isOurs ? '#fbbf24' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)');
    svg.appendChild(dot);
    var lb = document.createElementNS(NS, 'text');
    lb.setAttribute('x', cx + 10); lb.setAttribute('y', cy + 4);
    lb.setAttribute('fill', m.isOurs ? '#7dd3fc' : '#cbd5e1');
    lb.setAttribute('font-size', '9');
    lb.textContent = m.name;
    svg.appendChild(lb);
  });
  var star = document.createElementNS(NS, 'text');
  star.setAttribute('x', ox + 2*cw + 14);
  star.setAttribute('y', oy + 2*ch + 16);
  star.setAttribute('fill', '#fbbf24');
  star.setAttribute('font-size', '14');
  star.textContent = '★ This Work';
  svg.appendChild(star);
}

// ===== Init all on page load =====
window.addEventListener('load', function() {
  setTimeout(function() {
    if (typeof katex === 'undefined') {
      document.body.classList.add('katex-unavailable', 'katex-fallback');
      var warn = document.createElement('div');
      warn.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
      warn.className = 'katex-load-warn';
      warn.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:14px;right:14px;z-index:220;max-width:min(92vw,320px);padding:12px 14px;background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--orange);font-size:0.78rem;line-height:1.45;border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);';
      warn.innerHTML = '<strong>KaTeX did not load.</strong> Math may appear as raw <code>$...$</code>. Allow <code>cdn.jsdelivr.net</code> or bundle KaTeX locally.';
      document.body.appendChild(warn);
    }
    if (document.getElementById('gammaSlider')) updateGammaVis();
    updateActivationExplorer();
    updateWingExplorer();
    updateFocalExplorer();
    updateKendallExplorer();
    updateCBCalc();
    if (document.getElementById('channelGamma') || document.getElementById('channelGammaSlider')) updateChannelGrid();
    bindFilmController();
    initCmHeat16();
    if (document.getElementById('pyramidSlider')) updatePyramidSlider();
    if (document.getElementById('hmCanvas')) drawHeatmapSoftmax();
    if (document.getElementById('rfSelect')) updateReceptiveField();
    initLazyCharts();
    renderPoseVector();
    initRootNormalizationDemo();
    initNoveltyMatrix();
    initFilmMiniGrids();
    initKendallScaleBars();
    applyTermHighlight(0);
    showCbStage(1);
    var fp = document.getElementById('formal-problem-block');
    if (fp && typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') window.rerenderMath(fp);
  }, 200);
});


// ========== POPW v6: mode, speaker notes, present charts ==========
(function() {
  function setMode(mode) {
    if (mode !== 'present' && mode !== 'study') return;
    document.body.classList.add('mode-transitioning');
    document.body.setAttribute('data-mode', mode);
    var pBtn = document.getElementById('modePresentBtn');
    var sBtn = document.getElementById('modeStudyBtn');
    if (pBtn && sBtn) {
      pBtn.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'present');
      sBtn.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'study');
      pBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', mode === 'present' ? 'true' : 'false');
      sBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', mode === 'study' ? 'true' : 'false');
    }
    setTimeout(function() { document.body.classList.remove('mode-transitioning'); }, 320);
    var modeRoot = mode === 'study' ? document.getElementById('study-mode') : document.getElementById('present-mode');
    if (modeRoot && typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') {
      setTimeout(function() { window.rerenderMath(modeRoot); }, 100);
    }
  }
  window.setPopwMode = setMode;

  var dock = document.getElementById('speakerDock');
  var dockList = document.getElementById('speakerDockList');
  var speakerVisible = false;

  function updateSpeakerNotesForSlide() {
    if (!dock || !dockList) return;
    var slides = document.querySelectorAll('.pres-slide');
    var best = null;
    var bestDist = Infinity;
    var mid = window.innerHeight / 2;
    slides.forEach(function(sl) {
      var r = sl.getBoundingClientRect();
      var c = r.top + r.height / 2;
      var d = Math.abs(c - mid);
      if (r.bottom > 80 && r.top < window.innerHeight - 80 && d < bestDist) {
        bestDist = d;
        best = sl;
      }
    });
    dockList.innerHTML = '';
    if (!best) return;
    for (var i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
      var t = best.getAttribute('data-sn' + i);
      if (!t) continue;
      var li = document.createElement('li');
      li.textContent = t;
      dockList.appendChild(li);
    }
  }

  function toggleSpeaker() {
    if (document.body.getAttribute('data-mode') !== 'present') return;
    speakerVisible = !speakerVisible;
    if (dock) dock.hidden = !speakerVisible;
    if (speakerVisible) updateSpeakerNotesForSlide();
  }
  window.togglePopwSpeaker = toggleSpeaker;

  document.getElementById('modePresentBtn') && document.getElementById('modePresentBtn').addEventListener('click', function() { setMode('present'); });
  document.getElementById('modeStudyBtn') && document.getElementById('modeStudyBtn').addEventListener('click', function() { setMode('study'); });

  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (!e.key) return;
    var t = e.target;
    if (t && (t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.tagName === 'SELECT' || t.isContentEditable)) return;
    var k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (k === 'm') {
      e.preventDefault();
      setMode(document.body.getAttribute('data-mode') === 'present' ? 'study' : 'present');
    }
    if (k === 's') {
      e.preventDefault();
      toggleSpeaker();
    }
  });

  window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
    if (speakerVisible && document.body.getAttribute('data-mode') === 'present') updateSpeakerNotesForSlide();
  }, { passive: true });

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-pres-jump]').forEach(function(btn) {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
      var id = btn.getAttribute('data-pres-jump');
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    });
  });

  var dotObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
    entries.forEach(function(entry) {
      if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
      var id = entry.target.id;
      document.querySelectorAll('.pres-dot').forEach(function(d) {
        d.classList.toggle('active', d.getAttribute('data-pres-jump') === id);
      });
    });
  }, { rootMargin: '-45% 0px -45% 0px', threshold: 0 });
  document.querySelectorAll('.pres-slide').forEach(function(s) { dotObserver.observe(s); });

  function goStudy(hash) {
    setMode('study');
    if (hash) {
      setTimeout(function() {
        var el = document.querySelector(hash);
        if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
      }, 350);
    }
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('.pres-click-study').forEach(function(el) {
    el.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
      var h = el.getAttribute('data-study');
      if (h) { ev.preventDefault(); goStudy(h); }
    });
  });

  var filmBox = document.getElementById('pres-film-box');
  if (filmBox) {
    filmBox.style.cursor = 'pointer';
    filmBox.addEventListener('click', function() { goStudy('#film'); });
  }

  function buildPresGrid(containerId, baseBright) {
    var c = document.getElementById(containerId);
    if (!c) return;
    c.innerHTML = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      var d = document.createElement('div');
      d.className = 'pcell';
      var phase = (i * 17 + (baseBright ? 0 : 33)) % 100 / 100;
      d.style.opacity = 0.25 + phase * 0.35;
      c.appendChild(d);
    }
  }
  buildPresGrid('presGridOff', false);
  buildPresGrid('presGridOn', true);

  var poseSlider = document.getElementById('presPoseSlider');
  function updatePresFilmGrid() {
    var t = poseSlider ? parseInt(poseSlider.value, 10) / 100 : 0.5;
    var on = document.getElementById('presGridOn');
    if (!on) return;
    var cells = on.querySelectorAll('.pcell');
    cells.forEach(function(cell, i) {
      var wave = Math.sin((i / 64) * Math.PI * 2 + t * 6.28) * 0.5 + 0.5;
      var amp = 0.35 + wave * 0.55;
      cell.style.opacity = (0.2 + amp * (0.5 + t * 0.5)).toFixed(3);
      cell.style.filter = 'brightness(' + (0.85 + wave * 0.5 * (0.5 + t)) + ')';
    });
    var off = document.getElementById('presGridOff');
    if (off) {
      off.querySelectorAll('.pcell').forEach(function(cell) {
        cell.style.opacity = '0.42';
        cell.style.filter = 'none';
      });
    }
  }
  if (poseSlider) {
    poseSlider.addEventListener('input', updatePresFilmGrid);
    updatePresFilmGrid();
  }

  var presBar = null;
  function presBarChartThemeColors() {
    var light = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'light';
    return {
      tc: light ? '#5a5a5a' : '#8892a4',
      sub: light ? '#475569' : '#94a3b8',
      grid: light ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.1)' : 'rgba(128,128,128,0.12)'
    };
  }
  function updatePresBarChartTheme() {
    if (!presBar) return;
    var c = presBarChartThemeColors();
    presBar.options.plugins.title.color = c.tc;
    presBar.options.plugins.subtitle.color = c.sub;
    presBar.options.scales.x.grid.color = c.grid;
    presBar.options.scales.x.ticks.color = c.tc;
    presBar.options.scales.y.ticks.color = c.tc;
    presBar.update();
  }
  window.updatePresBarChartTheme = updatePresBarChartTheme;
  function initPresBarChart() {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('presBarChart');
    if (!canvas || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    var col = presBarChartThemeColors();
    var barData = [20.686175580222, 32.29061553985873, 37.91875, 37.41875];
    var pm = window.POPW_METRICS && window.POPW_METRICS.models;
    if (pm && pm.improved3 && pm.improved4_film) {
      barData = [
        pm.improved3.act_top1 * 100,
        pm.improved3_film.act_top1 * 100,
        pm.improved4.act_top1 * 100,
        pm.improved4_film.act_top1 * 100
      ];
    }
    presBar = new Chart(canvas.getContext('2d'), {
      type: 'bar',
      data: {
        labels: ['improved3', 'improved3_film', 'improved4', 'improved4_film'],
        datasets: [{
          label: 'Activity Top-1 %',
          data: barData,
          backgroundColor: ['rgba(136,146,164,0.7)', 'rgba(56,189,248,0.75)', 'rgba(74,222,128,0.65)', 'rgba(56,189,248,0.55)'],
          borderColor: ['#8892a4', '#38bdf8', '#4ade80', '#38bdf8'],
          borderWidth: 1
        }]
      },
      options: {
        indexAxis: 'y',
        responsive: true,
        plugins: {
          legend: { display: false },
          title: { display: true, text: 'Activity Top-1 % (from window.POPW_METRICS / compare_results.json)', color: col.tc, font: { size: 11 } },
          subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Data: act_top1 × 100; detection mAP not shown', color: col.sub, font: { size: 9 } }
        },
        scales: {
          x: {
            min: 0,
            max: 45,
            grid: { color: col.grid },
            ticks: { color: col.tc, callback: function(v) { return v + '%'; } }
          },
          y: { grid: { display: false }, ticks: { color: col.tc } }
        }
      }
    });
  }

  function drawPresLossCanvases() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.pres-loss-canvas').forEach(function(cv) {
      var kind = cv.getAttribute('data-loss');
      var ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
      var w = cv.width = cv.parentElement.clientWidth || 280;
      var h = cv.height = 120;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(136,146,164,0.4)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(30, h - 24);
      ctx.lineTo(w - 10, h - 24);
      ctx.stroke();
      if (kind === 'wing') {
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (var x = 0; x < w - 40; x++) {
          var u = x / (w - 50);
          var e = 0.02 + u * 0.5;
          var yL2 = Math.min(e * e * 80, h - 30);
          var yW = Math.min(Math.log(1 + e / 0.01) * 12, h - 30);
          var px = 30 + x;
          if (x === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, h - 24 - yW);
          else ctx.lineTo(px, h - 24 - yW);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#f87171';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (var x2 = 0; x2 < w - 40; x2++) {
          var u2 = x2 / (w - 50);
          var e2 = 0.02 + u2 * 0.5;
          var yL = Math.min(e2 * e2 * 80, h - 30);
          var px2 = 30 + x2;
          if (x2 === 0) ctx.moveTo(px2, h - 24 - yL);
          else ctx.lineTo(px2, h - 24 - yL);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
      } else if (kind === 'cb') {
        for (var i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
          var bw = (w - 60) / 8;
          var freq = i < 2 ? 0.92 : (i < 5 ? 0.45 : 0.12);
          ctx.fillStyle = i < 2 ? 'rgba(251,146,60,0.75)' : 'rgba(136,146,164,0.45)';
          ctx.fillRect(35 + i * bw, h - 24 - freq * (h - 50), bw - 4, freq * (h - 50));
        }
      } else {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(136,146,164,0.5)';
        ctx.fillRect(40, 30, 50, h - 60);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(251,146,60,0.5)';
        ctx.fillRect(110, 50, 50, h - 80);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(56,189,248,0.5)';
        ctx.fillRect(180, 40, 50, h - 70);
        ctx.fillText('scale mismatch', w / 2 - 40, 18);
      }
    });
  }

  var presStepIndex = 0;
  var presStepBodies = ["<p><strong>1. Input.</strong> RGB frame + 51D pose (demo values).</p><p class=\"mono\">wrist norm (0.62, 0.41), shoulder (0.55, 0.38), spine tilt 0.71</p>", "<p><strong>2. PoseFiLMModule MLPs.</strong> Separate <span class=\"mono\">gamma_net</span> and <span class=\"mono\">beta_net</span>: Linear(51\u2192512)+ReLU+Linear(512\u21922048) each. <strong>\u03b3 = 1+tanh</strong> \u2208 (0,2); <strong>\u03b2</strong> is <em>unbounded</em> (no tanh).</p><p class=\"mono\">Sample \u03b3: 1.08, 0.94, 1.21 &nbsp; \u03b2: 0.03, \u22120.11, 0.07</p>", "<p><strong>3. FiLM on C5.</strong></p><p class=\"film-term\">$$\\gamma_i \\cdot F_i + \\beta_i$$</p><p>Per channel on <span class=\"mono\">[B,2048,20,15]</span> (640\u00d7480 POPW input).</p>", "<p><strong>4. Losses (illustrative).</strong></p><p class=\"mono\">L_det, L_pose Wing, L_act CB-Focal</p>", "<p><strong>5. Kendall total.</strong> <span class=\"mono\">exp(\u2212lv) L + lv</span> per task (<code>log_var_det</code>, <code>log_var_pose</code>, <code>log_var_act</code>), <span class=\"mono\">lv</span> clamped [\u22124,2] in <code>losses.py</code>.</p>"];

  function renderPresStep() {
    var body = document.getElementById('presStepBody');
    var label = document.getElementById('presStepLabel');
    if (!body || !label) return;
    body.innerHTML = presStepBodies[presStepIndex] || '';
    label.textContent = (presStepIndex + 1) + ' / ' + presStepBodies.length;
    if (typeof renderMathInElement === 'function') {
      try { renderMathInElement(body, {delimiters:[{left:'\\(',right:'\\)',display:false},{left:'$$',right:'$$',display:true}]}); } catch (e) {}
    }
    body.querySelectorAll('.term-highlight').forEach(function(el) { el.classList.remove('term-highlight'); });
    if (presStepIndex === 2) {
      var ft = body.querySelector('.film-term');
      if (ft) ft.classList.add('term-highlight');
    }
  }

  document.getElementById('presNext') && document.getElementById('presNext').addEventListener('click', function() {
    if (presStepIndex < presStepBodies.length - 1) { presStepIndex++; renderPresStep(); }
  });
  document.getElementById('presPrev') && document.getElementById('presPrev').addEventListener('click', function() {
    if (presStepIndex > 0) { presStepIndex--; renderPresStep(); }
  });
  renderPresStep();

  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (document.body.getAttribute('data-mode') !== 'present') return;
    var stepper = document.getElementById('presStepper');
    if (!stepper) return;
    var r = stepper.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.top > window.innerHeight || r.bottom < 0) return;
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); document.getElementById('presNext').click(); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); document.getElementById('presPrev').click(); }
  });

  function initPresentChartsLazy() {
    var targets = [
      { id: 'presBarChart', init: initPresBarChart }
    ];
    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      targets.forEach(function(t) { if (document.getElementById(t.id)) t.init(); });
      return;
    }
    var seen = Object.create(null);
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
      entries.forEach(function(entry) {
        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
        var id = entry.target.id;
        if (seen[id]) return;
        var t = targets.find(function(x) { return x.id === id; });
        if (!t) return;
        seen[id] = true;
        try { t.init(); } catch (e) {}
        io.unobserve(entry.target);
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.15, rootMargin: '200px 0px' });
    targets.forEach(function(t) {
      var el = document.getElementById(t.id);
      if (el) io.observe(el);
    });
  }

  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    setTimeout(function() {
      drawPresLossCanvases();
      initPresentChartsLazy();
      if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined' && lucide.createIcons) lucide.createIcons();
    }, 200);
  });
})();

  function pct(x, nd) {
    nd = nd || 2;
    return (x * 100).toFixed(nd) + '%';
  }

  function buildPres8Grid() {
    var c = document.getElementById('pres8GridMetaphor');
    if (!c) return;
    c.innerHTML = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
      var row = Math.floor(i / 16);
      var col = i % 16;
      var base = 0.6;
      if (i <= 30) base = 0.10 + ((i % 7) * 0.03);
      else if (i <= 60) base = 0.15 + ((i % 6) * 0.03);
      else if ((i >= 180 && i <= 210) || (i >= 230 && i <= 255)) base = 0.85 + (((i * 13) % 100) / 100) * 0.9;
      else base = 0.40 + (((row + col) % 8) / 8) * 0.70;
      var d = document.createElement('div');
      d.className = 'pres8-cell';
      d.dataset.i = String(i);
      d.dataset.baseGamma = base.toFixed(4);
      d.title = 'Cell ' + i + ' · base ≈ ' + base.toFixed(2);
      c.appendChild(d);
    }
    updatePres8FilmGrid();
  }

  function updatePres8FilmGrid() {
    var gEl = document.getElementById('pres8GammaSlider');
    var bEl = document.getElementById('pres8BetaSlider');
    var g = gEl ? parseFloat(gEl.value) : 1;
    var b = bEl ? parseFloat(bEl.value) : 0;
    var cells = document.querySelectorAll('#pres8GridMetaphor .pres8-cell');
    cells.forEach(function(cell, idx) {
      var base = parseFloat(cell.dataset.baseGamma || '0.6');
      var bShift = b * 0.22;
      var eff = Math.max(0, Math.min(2.5, base * g + bShift));
      if (eff > 1.3) {
        cell.style.background = 'rgba(56,189,248,' + ((eff - 1) * 0.5).toFixed(3) + ')';
        cell.style.boxShadow = '0 0 4px rgba(56,189,248,0.55)';
        cell.style.border = '1px solid rgba(56,189,248,0.5)';
      } else if (eff < 0.4) {
        cell.style.background = 'rgba(10,15,30,0.92)';
        cell.style.boxShadow = 'none';
        cell.style.border = '1px solid rgba(71,85,105,0.35)';
      } else {
        cell.style.background = 'rgba(136,146,164,' + (eff * 0.32).toFixed(3) + ')';
        cell.style.boxShadow = 'none';
        cell.style.border = '1px solid rgba(100,116,139,0.25)';
      }
      cell.title = 'Cell ' + idx + ' · base γ≈' + base.toFixed(2) + ' · effective≈' + eff.toFixed(2);
    });
  }
  var gs = document.getElementById('pres8GammaSlider');
  var bs = document.getElementById('pres8BetaSlider');
  if (gs) gs.addEventListener('input', updatePres8FilmGrid);
  if (bs) bs.addEventListener('input', updatePres8FilmGrid);
  buildPres8Grid();

  var CLASS_LABELS = [
    'Lay (frame)', 'Attach (leg)', 'Flip', 'Lay (back)', 'Pick up',
    'Tighten (screwdriver)', 'Attach (top)', 'Lay (front)', 'Tighten (hand)',
    'Sit down', 'Stand up', 'Insert dowel', 'Hammer', 'Rotate',
    'Spin (screwdriver)', 'NA'
  ];
  var CLASS_COUNTS = [
    520, 380, 340, 310, 285, 248, 215, 195, 178, 162, 148, 135, 98, 76, 58, 52
  ];
  (function sortClassDist() {
    var pairs = CLASS_LABELS.map(function(l, i) { return { l: l, c: CLASS_COUNTS[i] }; });
    pairs.sort(function(a, b) { return b.c - a.c; });
    CLASS_LABELS = pairs.map(function(p) { return p.l; });
    CLASS_COUNTS = pairs.map(function(p) { return p.c; });
  })();
  var CLASS_SUM = CLASS_COUNTS.reduce(function(a, b) { return a + b; }, 0);
  var CLASS_MAX = Math.max.apply(null, CLASS_COUNTS);
  var CLASS_MIN = Math.min.apply(null, CLASS_COUNTS.filter(function(c) { return c > 0; }));

  function initPres8ClassDist() {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('pres8ClassDist');
    if (!canvas || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    destroyChartIfAny(canvas);
    var wrap = canvas.closest('.pres8-chart-canvas-wrap');
    if (wrap) {
      var n = CLASS_LABELS.length;
      var h = Math.min(980, Math.max(440, n * 19 + 140));
      wrap.style.minHeight = h + 'px';
    }
    var tc = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'light' ? '#5a5a5a' : '#8892a4';
    var colors = CLASS_COUNTS.map(function(c) {
      if (c === CLASS_MAX) return 'rgba(74,85,104,0.85)';
      if (c <= CLASS_MIN * 1.5) return 'rgba(248,113,113,0.85)';
      if (c < 200) return 'rgba(251,146,60,0.85)';
      return 'rgba(136,146,164,0.55)';
    });
    new Chart(canvas.getContext('2d'), {
      type: 'bar',
      data: {
        labels: CLASS_LABELS,
        datasets: [{ label: 'Grouped frames', data: CLASS_COUNTS, backgroundColor: colors, borderWidth: 0, barThickness: 11, maxBarThickness: 16 }]
      },
      options: {
        indexAxis: 'y',
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        layout: { padding: { left: 4, right: 12, top: 8, bottom: 6 } },
        plugins: {
          legend: { display: false },
          title: { display: true, text: 'Grouped 16-way class counts (sum ' + CLASS_SUM + ', sorted desc.)', color: tc, font: { size: 12, weight: '600', family: 'system-ui, sans-serif' } },
          subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Illustrative pedagogical counts — not official dataset statistics', color: tc, font: { size: 10 }, opacity: 0.85 },
          tooltip: {
            callbacks: {
              title: function(items) { return items.length ? items[0].label : ''; }
            }
          }
        },
        scales: {
          x: {
            ticks: { color: tc, font: { size: 10 } },
            grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.1)' },
            title: { display: true, text: 'Count', color: tc, font: { size: 10 } }
          },
          y: {
            ticks: { color: tc, font: { size: 10, family: 'ui-monospace, Menlo, monospace' }, autoSkip: false, maxTicksLimit: 60 },
            grid: { display: false },
            afterFit: function(scale) { scale.width = Math.max(scale.width, 152); }
          }
        }
      }
    });
  }

          legend: { display: false },
          title: { display: true, text: 'Max:min ratio = ' + (CLASS_MAX / CLASS_MIN).toFixed(1) + ':1', color: tc, font: { size: 12, weight: '600', family: 'system-ui, sans-serif' } },
          subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Long tail motivates CB-Focal (next slide)', color: tc, font: { size: 10 }, opacity: 0.85 },
          tooltip: {
            callbacks: {
              title: function(items) { return items.length ? items[0].label : ''; }
            }
          }
        },
        scales: {
          x: { ticks: { color: tc, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.1)' } },
          y: {
            ticks: { color: tc, font: { size: 10, family: 'ui-monospace, Menlo, monospace' }, autoSkip: false, maxTicksLimit: 60 },
            grid: { display: false },
            afterFit: function(scale) { scale.width = Math.max(scale.width, 152); }
          }
        }
      }
    });
  }

  function wingLoss(x, w, eps) {
    var ax = Math.abs(x);
    if (ax < w) return w * Math.log(1 + ax / eps);
    var C = w - w * Math.log(1 + w / eps);
    return ax - C;
  }
  function huber(x, delta) {
    var ax = Math.abs(x);
    if (ax <= delta) return 0.5 * x * x;
    return delta * (ax - 0.5 * delta);
  }

  function initPres8WingChart() {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('pres8WingCompare');
    if (!canvas || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    var w = 0.05, eps = 0.005, delta = 0.01;
    var xs = [];
    var n = 80;
    for (var i = 0; i <= n; i++) xs.push(-0.04 + (0.08 * i) / n);
    var l2 = xs.map(function(x) { return x * x; });
    var l1 = xs.map(function(x) { return Math.abs(x); });
    var hb = xs.map(function(x) { return huber(x, delta); });
    var wg = xs.map(function(x) { return wingLoss(x, w, eps); });
    var tc = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'light' ? '#1a1a1a' : '#e8eaf0';
    var ch = new Chart(canvas.getContext('2d'), {
      type: 'line',
      data: {
        labels: xs.map(function(x) { return x.toFixed(3); }),
        datasets: [
          { label: 'L2', data: l2, borderColor: '#f87171', pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.1, hidden: false },
          { label: 'L1', data: l1, borderColor: '#fb923c', pointRadius: 0, tension: 0, hidden: false },
          { label: 'Huber δ=0.01', data: hb, borderColor: '#a78bfa', pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.1, hidden: true },
          { label: 'Wing w=0.05 ε=0.005', data: wg, borderColor: '#38bdf8', borderWidth: 2.5, pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.1, hidden: false }
        ]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: {
          legend: { labels: { color: tc } },
          title: { display: true, text: 'Loss L(x) vs normalized coordinate error', color: tc, font: { size: 12 } },
          subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Present deck: loss curves. Study §7 wing chart: dL/dx (gradients) with same w, ε.', color: tc, font: { size: 10 }, opacity: 0.75, padding: { bottom: 4 } }
        },
        scales: {
          x: { title: { display: true, text: 'Normalized error', color: tc }, ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 8, color: tc }, grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.06)' } },
          y: { title: { display: true, text: 'Loss', color: tc }, ticks: { color: tc }, grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.06)' } }
        }
      }
    });
    window.__pres8WingChart = ch;
  }

  // VIZ-CHECK FIX [VIZ-19]: Wing ∂L/∂x vs x; vertical line at ω = WING_OMEGA
  function initPres8WingGradChart() {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('pres8WingGrad');
    if (!canvas || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    var w = 0.05;
    var eps = 0.005;
    var delta = 0.01;
    var xs = [];
    var n = 100;
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i <= n; i++) xs.push(-0.06 + (0.12 * i) / n);
    function wingDeriv(x) {
      var ax = Math.abs(x);
      if (ax < w) return (w / (eps + ax)) * (x >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
      return x >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
    function l2d(x) { return 2 * x; }
    function l1d(x) { return x >= 0 ? 1 : -1; }
    function huberd(x) {
      var ax = Math.abs(x);
      if (ax <= delta) return x;
      return x >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
    function xy(f) {
      return xs.map(function(x) { return { x: x, y: f(x) }; });
    }
    var tc = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'light' ? '#1a1a1a' : '#e8eaf0';
    new Chart(canvas.getContext('2d'), {
      type: 'line',
      data: {
        datasets: [
          { label: 'L2′', data: xy(l2d), borderColor: '#f87171', pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.1, parsing: false },
          { label: 'L1′', data: xy(l1d), borderColor: '#fb923c', pointRadius: 0, tension: 0, parsing: false },
          { label: 'Huber′ δ=0.01', data: xy(huberd), borderColor: '#a78bfa', pointRadius: 0, tension: 0, hidden: true, parsing: false },
          { label: 'Wing′ w=0.05', data: xy(wingDeriv), borderColor: '#38bdf8', borderWidth: 2.5, pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.15, parsing: false }
        ]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: {
          legend: { labels: { color: tc } },
          title: { display: true, text: '∂L/∂x vs normalized error (dashed: |x| = ω)', color: tc, font: { size: 12 } },
          subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Wing inner region: ∂/∂x w ln(1+|x|/ε) = ±w/(ε+|x|)', color: tc, font: { size: 10 }, opacity: 0.75 }
        },
        scales: {
          x: {
            type: 'linear',
            title: { display: true, text: 'x', color: tc },
            min: -0.06,
            max: 0.06,
            ticks: { color: tc },
            grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.06)' }
          },
          y: { title: { display: true, text: '∂L/∂x', color: tc }, ticks: { color: tc }, grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.06)' } }
        }
      },
      plugins: [{
        id: 'wingOmegaVLine',
        afterDraw: function(chart) {
          var ctx = chart.ctx;
          var xScale = chart.scales.x;
          var top = chart.chartArea.top;
          var bot = chart.chartArea.bottom;
          var x0 = xScale.getPixelForValue(w);
          if (!isFinite(x0)) return;
          ctx.save();
          ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(251,146,60,0.9)';
          ctx.lineWidth = 2;
          ctx.setLineDash([5, 4]);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(x0, top);
          ctx.lineTo(x0, bot);
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.setLineDash([]);
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(251,146,60,0.95)';
          ctx.font = '600 10px system-ui,sans-serif';
          ctx.fillText('ω=0.05', Math.min(x0 + 4, chart.chartArea.right - 48), top + 12);
          ctx.restore();
        }
      }]
    });
  }

  // VIZ-CHECK FIX [VIZ-24]: Kendall e^{-s}L + s vs s (teaching; L_t fixed for shape intuition)
  function initPres8KendallSigmaChart() {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('pres8KendallSigma');
    if (!canvas || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    var ss = [];
    for (var j = 0; j <= 80; j++) ss.push(-4 + (6 * j) / 80);
    var Ldet = 2.4;
    var Lpose = 0.04;
    var Lact = 0.18;
    function row(L) {
      return ss.map(function(s) {
        return Math.exp(-s) * L + s;
      });
    }
    var tc = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'light' ? '#1a1a1a' : '#e8eaf0';
    new Chart(canvas.getContext('2d'), {
      type: 'line',
      data: {
        labels: ss.map(function(s) { return s.toFixed(2); }),
        datasets: [
          { label: 'det (L≈2.4)', data: row(Ldet), borderColor: '#fb923c', borderWidth: 2, pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.2 },
          { label: 'pose Wing (L≈0.04)', data: row(Lpose), borderColor: '#38bdf8', borderWidth: 2, pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.2 },
          { label: 'act CB-Focal (L≈0.18)', data: row(Lact), borderColor: '#fbbf24', borderWidth: 2, pointRadius: 0, tension: 0.2 }
        ]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: {
          legend: { labels: { color: tc } },
          title: { display: true, text: 'e^{−s}L + s vs s (each task, illustrative L)', color: tc, font: { size: 12 } },
          subtitle: { display: true, text: 'Minima shift with L; s clamped to [−4,2] in losses.MultiTaskLoss', color: tc, font: { size: 10 }, opacity: 0.8 }
        },
        scales: {
          x: { title: { display: true, text: 's = clamp(log σ²)', color: tc }, ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 10, color: tc }, grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.06)' } },
          y: { title: { display: true, text: 'Kendall term', color: tc }, ticks: { color: tc }, grid: { color: 'rgba(128,128,128,0.06)' } }
        }
      }
    });
  }
