  function initPres8FocalCurve() {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('pres8FocalCurve');
    if (!canvas || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    var ps = [];
    for (var i = 0; i <= 50; i++) ps.push(i / 50);
    function series(gam) {
      return ps.map(function(p) { return Math.pow(1 - p, gam); });
    }
    var tc = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'light' ? '#1a1a1a' : '#e8eaf0';

        }
      }
    });
  }

  function cbAlpha(nc, beta) {
    beta = beta || 0.9999;
    if (nc <= 0) return NaN;
    return (1 - beta) / (1 - Math.pow(beta, nc));
  }

  function wirePres8CbCalc() {
    var inp = document.getElementById('pres8NcIn');
    var out = document.getElementById('pres8AlphaOut');
    if (!inp || !out) return;
    function go() {
      var n = parseFloat(inp.value, 10);
      if (!isFinite(n) || n < 1) {
        n = 1;
        inp.value = '1';
      }
      var a = cbAlpha(n, 0.9999);
      out.textContent = isFinite(a) ? a.toFixed(6) : '—';
    }
    inp.addEventListener('input', go);
    go();
  }

  var pres8Idx = 0;
  var REDUCE_MOTION = !!(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
  var pres8Bodies = ["<p class=\"pres-one-line\" style=\"font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin:0 0 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);padding-bottom:8px\">Teaching trace: keyframes and scalars are <strong>illustrative</strong> unless explicitly tied to <code>compare_results.json</code> (§9).</p><p class=\"pres7-sec-label\">Step 0 \u00b7 Scene</p><p>Activity <strong>insert_dowel</strong>: forward bend, right arm extended toward furniture (illustrative keyframe).</p><p class=\"mono\">IKEA LACK-style flat pack; right hip root (0,0) reference.</p>", "<p class=\"pres7-sec-label\">Step 2 \u00b7 Pose MLP</p><p><span class=\"mono\">gamma_net</span> / <span class=\"mono\">beta_net</span>: 51\u2192512\u2192ReLU\u21922048; down-weights easy frame.</p>", "<p class=\"pres7-sec-label\">Step 6 \u00b7 Kendall total</p><p>$$\\mathcal{L}=\\sum_{t\\in\\{d,p,a\\}}\\bigl(e^{-s_t}\\mathcal{L}_t+s_t\\bigr),\\quad s_t=\\mathrm{clamp}(\\log\\sigma_t^2,-4,2)$$</p><p class=\"mono\">Illustrative: s_d=\u22120.5, s_p=0, s_a=0.3 reweights det vs pose vs act each step.</p><p>Backprop updates FiLM and heads jointly.</p>"];

  function renderPres8Step() {
    var body = document.getElementById('pres8StepBody');
    var label = document.getElementById('pres8StepLabel');
    if (!body || !label) return;
    body.innerHTML = pres8Bodies[pres8Idx] || '';
    label.textContent = (pres8Idx + 1) + ' / ' + pres8Bodies.length;
    if (typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') {
      setTimeout(function() { window.rerenderMath(body); }, 0);
    }
  }
  document.getElementById('pres8Next') && document.getElementById('pres8Next').addEventListener('click', function() {
    if (pres8Idx < pres8Bodies.length - 1) { pres8Idx++; renderPres8Step(); }
  });
  document.getElementById('pres8Prev') && document.getElementById('pres8Prev').addEventListener('click', function() {
    if (pres8Idx > 0) { pres8Idx--; renderPres8Step(); }
  });
  renderPres8Step();

  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (document.body.getAttribute('data-mode') !== 'present') return;
    var stepper = document.getElementById('pres8Stepper');
    if (!stepper) return;
    var r = stepper.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.top > window.innerHeight || r.bottom < 0) return;
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); document.getElementById('pres8Next').click(); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); document.getElementById('pres8Prev').click(); }
  });

  function jumpPres8(n) {
    var el = document.getElementById('pres8-' + n);
    if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  }
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (document.body.getAttribute('data-mode') !== 'present') return;
    var t = e.target;
    if (t && (t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.tagName === 'SELECT' || t.isContentEditable)) return;
    if (!/^Digit[0-9]$/.test(e.code)) return;
    var d = parseInt(e.code.replace('Digit', ''), 10);
    if (d === 0) jumpPres8(9);
    else if (d >= 1 && d <= 9) jumpPres8(d - 1);
  });

  function animateMetric(el, target, suffix, dur) {
    if (!el) return;
    if (REDUCE_MOTION) { el.textContent = target + (suffix || ''); return; }
    dur = dur || 900;
    var start = 0;
    var startT = null;
    var tgt = parseFloat(target);
    function frame(now) {
      if (!startT) startT = now;
      var p = Math.min(1, (now - startT) / dur);
      var eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);
      var v = start + (tgt - start) * eased;
      el.textContent = v.toFixed(2) + (suffix || '');
      if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      else el.textContent = target + (suffix || '');
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  function initPres8MetricAnim() {
    function runOne(el) {
      if (!el || el.dataset.done) return;
      var tr = el.getAttribute('data-count-to');
      var suf = el.getAttribute('data-count-suffix') || '';
      if (!tr) return;
      // VIZ-CHECK FIX [VIZ-01]: apply count-up animation class once
      el.classList.add('metric-number');
      el.dataset.done = '1';
      animateMetric(el, tr, suf, 800);
    }
    var obs = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
      entries.forEach(function(en) {
        if (!en.isIntersecting) return;
        runOne(en.target);
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.35 });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-count-to]').forEach(function(el) { obs.observe(el); });
    window.addEventListener('load', function() {
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-count-to]').forEach(function(el) {
        if (el.dataset.done) return;
        var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (r.top < window.innerHeight && r.bottom > 0) runOne(el);
      });
    });
  }

  function initPres8ChartsLazy() {
    var targets = [
      { id: 'pres8ClassDist', init: initPres8ClassDist },
      { id: 'pres8ClassDist2', init: initPres8ClassDist2 },
      { id: 'pres8WingCompare', init: function() { initPres8WingChart(); wirePres8WingToggles(); initPres8WingGradChart(); } },
      { id: 'pres8FocalCurve', init: initPres8FocalCurve },
      { id: 'pres8KendallSigma', init: initPres8KendallSigmaChart },
      { id: 'pres8AblationChart', init: initPres8Ablation }
    ];
    if (!('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      targets.forEach(function(t) { if (document.getElementById(t.id)) t.init(); });
      wirePres8CbCalc();
      initPres8MetricAnim();
      return;
    }
    var seen = Object.create(null);
    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
      entries.forEach(function(entry) {
        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
        var id = entry.target.id;
        if (seen[id]) return;
        var t = targets.find(function(x) { return x.id === id; });
        if (!t) return;
        seen[id] = true;
        try { t.init(); } catch (e) {}
        io.unobserve(entry.target);
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.15, rootMargin: '200px 0px' });
    targets.forEach(function(t) {
      var el = document.getElementById(t.id);
      if (el) io.observe(el);
    });
    wirePres8CbCalc();
    initPres8MetricAnim();
  }

  /* K1 FIX: animated Kendall scale bars trigger on scroll into §8 */
  (function() {
    var barTargets = [
      { id: 'kBar1', width: '100%' },   /* L_det  ≈ 2.4  = 100% */
      { id: 'kBar3', width:   '7.5%' }, /* L_act  ≈ 0.18 = 7.5% */
      { id: 'kBar2', width:   '1.7%' }  /* L_pose ≈ 0.04 = 1.7% */
    ];
    var animated = false;
    function animKendall() {
      if (animated) return;
      animated = true;
      barTargets.forEach(function(b) {
        var el = document.getElementById(b.id);
        if (el) el.style.width = b.width;
      });
    }
    if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
      var kObs = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
        entries.forEach(function(entry) {
          if (entry.isIntersecting) { setTimeout(animKendall, 180); kObs.disconnect(); }
        });
      }, { threshold: 0.25 });
      var kSec = document.getElementById('pres8-8');
      if (kSec) kObs.observe(kSec);
    } else {
      animKendall();
    }
  })();

  function refreshMetricsFromRemote() {
    if (typeof fetch !== 'function') return;
    fetch('compare_results.json', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(function(r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : Promise.reject(new Error('fetch')); })
      .then(function(data) {
        if (!data || !data.models) return;
        METRICS = data;
        window.POPW_METRICS = data;
        var canvas = document.getElementById('pres8AblationChart');
        if (canvas && typeof Chart !== 'undefined' && Chart.getChart && Chart.getChart(canvas)) {
          destroyChartIfAny(canvas);
          initPres8Ablation();
        }
      })
      .catch(function() { /* keep inlined snapshot */ });
  }

  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    setTimeout(function() {
      initPres8ChartsLazy();
      refreshMetricsFromRemote();
    }, 200);
  });
})();

// ===== Close mobile sidebar on link click =====
navLinks.forEach(function(link) {
  link.addEventListener('click', function() {
    document.querySelector('.sidebar').classList.remove('open');
  });
});
</script>

<script>
/* ========== POPW v10 LivingFrame — all audit fixes applied ========== */
(function() {
  'use strict';

  /* ---- FIX 16: Theme-aware colors for canvas ---- */
  function getThemeColors() {
    var style = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    return {
      teal:   style.getPropertyValue('--teal').trim()   || '#38bdf8',
      orange: style.getPropertyValue('--orange').trim() || '#fb923c',
      green:  style.getPropertyValue('--green').trim()  || '#4ade80',
      gold:   style.getPropertyValue('--gold').trim()   || '#fbbf24',
      bg:     style.getPropertyValue('--bg').trim()     || '#0d1117',
      text:   style.getPropertyValue('--text').trim()   || '#e8eaf0',
      muted:  style.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#8892a4',
    };
  }

  var JOINTS = [
    { id:'j0',  name:'Nose',            x:240,y:42,  coords:[0.00,0.12,-0.05], dim:false },
    { id:'j1',  name:'Left Eye',        x:233,y:37,  coords:[-0.03,0.14,-0.05], dim:true  },
    { id:'j2',  name:'Right Eye',       x:247,y:37,  coords:[0.03,0.14,-0.05], dim:true  },
    { id:'j3',  name:'Left Ear',        x:225,y:42,  coords:[-0.07,0.12,-0.04], dim:true  },
    { id:'j4',  name:'Right Ear',       x:255,y:42,  coords:[0.07,0.12,-0.04], dim:true  },
    { id:'j5',  name:'Left Shoulder',   x:195,y:80,  coords:[-0.18,0.35,0.08], key:true  },
    { id:'j6',  name:'Right Shoulder',  x:285,y:80,  coords:[0.18,0.35,0.10], key:true  },
    { id:'j7',  name:'Left Elbow',      x:170,y:115, coords:[-0.22,0.52,0.18] },
    { id:'j8',  name:'Right Elbow',     x:312,y:115, coords:[0.26,0.55,0.25] },
    { id:'j9',  name:'Left Wrist',      x:168,y:152, coords:[-0.23,0.71,0.38], wrist:true },
    { id:'j10', name:'Right Wrist',     x:318,y:152, coords:[0.28,0.71,0.42], wrist:true },
    { id:'j11', name:'Left Hip (ROOT)', x:210,y:145, coords:[0.00,0.00,0.00], root:true  },
    { id:'j12', name:'Right Hip',       x:270,y:145, coords:[0.06,0.00,0.02], root:true  },
    { id:'j13', name:'Left Knee',       x:198,y:195, coords:[-0.08,0.52,0.05] },
    { id:'j14', name:'Right Knee',      x:282,y:195, coords:[0.10,0.52,0.06] },
    { id:'j15', name:'Left Ankle',      x:192,y:240, coords:[-0.09,0.96,0.04] },
    { id:'j16', name:'Right Ankle',     x:290,y:240, coords:[0.11,0.96,0.05] }
  ];

  var FEATURE_MAPS = {
    c1: { label:'Stem + maxpool', tensor:'[64 × 160 × 120]', color:'#3b82f6',
      desc:'layer0 in model.py (conv7×7, BN, ReLU, maxpool) — ¼ resolution vs 640×480.' },
    c2: { label:'layer1 (ResNet C2)', tensor:'[256 × 160 × 120]', color:'#6366f1',
      desc:'First residual stage — edges and local textures.' },
    c3: { label:'layer2 (ResNet C3)', tensor:'[512 × 80 × 60]', color:'#8b5cf6',
      desc:'Stride-8 map — mid-level parts.' },
    c4: { label:'layer3 (ResNet C4)', tensor:'[1024 × 40 × 30]', color:'#a855f7',
      desc:'Stride-16 — feeds FPN lateral_c3 in model.py.' },
    c5: { label:'layer4 (ResNet C5)', tensor:'[2048 × 20 × 15]', color:'#38bdf8',
      desc:'Stride-32 — PoseFiLMModule modulates this tensor before the activity head.' }
  };

  var DESC = {
    rgb: 'Raw RGB tensor for POPW (640×480). This 16:9 teaching proxy matches the idea of the spatial input plane; backbone uses ImageNet normalization.',
    skeleton: 'Pose vector p ∈ ℝ⁵¹: normalized (x,y) per joint plus confidences. Hover each joint for coordinates.',
    c1: FEATURE_MAPS.c1.desc,
    c2: FEATURE_MAPS.c2.desc,
    c3: FEATURE_MAPS.c3.desc,
    c4: FEATURE_MAPS.c4.desc,
    c5: FEATURE_MAPS.c5.desc,
    film: 'FiLM applies γ·C5+β per channel. Drag γ and β on panels that expose sliders; teal = amplified, dark = suppressed.',
    loss: 'Wing loss: small errors get log-shaped gradients; outliers cap linearly. GT vs pred wrist shown in frame space.'
  };

  function hexToRgb(hex) {
    var n = hex.replace('#','');
    return { r: parseInt(n.slice(0,2),16), g: parseInt(n.slice(2,4),16), b: parseInt(n.slice(4,6),16) };
  }

  /* ---- FIX 3: Anatomically correct feature map activations (no Math.random) ---- */
  var C5_ACTIVATIONS = [
    [0.10, 0.10, 0.20, 0.20, 0.30, 0.10, 0.10],
    [0.10, 0.30, 0.50, 0.60, 0.40, 0.20, 0.10],
    [0.20, 0.40, 0.70, 0.80, 0.70, 0.40, 0.20],
    [0.20, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90, 0.80, 0.50, 0.20],
    [0.10, 0.30, 0.50, 0.60, 0.90, 0.60, 0.30],
    [0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.40, 0.30, 0.10],
    [0.10, 0.10, 0.20, 0.20, 0.20, 0.10, 0.10]
  ];

  function getC1Activation(row, col, rows, cols) {
    var x = col / cols * 480, y = row / rows * 270;
    var nearTorso  = Math.abs(x-240) < 50 && y > 70  && y < 155;
    var nearArms   = (Math.abs(x-170) < 15 && y > 75  && y < 155) ||
                     (Math.abs(x-312) < 15 && y > 75  && y < 155);
    var nearLegs   = (Math.abs(x-195) < 12 && y > 145 && y < 245) ||
                     (Math.abs(x-282) < 12 && y > 145 && y < 245);
    var nearShoulders = Math.abs(y-80) < 8 && x > 170 && x < 310;
    var nearHips   = Math.abs(y-145) < 8 && x > 190 && x < 290;
    if(nearShoulders || nearHips) return 0.45 + Math.sin(x*0.1)*0.15;
    if(nearTorso || nearArms || nearLegs) return 0.25 + Math.sin(x*0.2+y*0.3)*0.18;
    return 0.01;
  }

  function getC2Activation(row, col, rows, cols) {
    var x = col / cols * 480, y = row / rows * 270;
    var inBody = x>=170 && x<=320 && y>=50 && y<=245;
    var inArms = (x>=140 && x<=180 && y>=75 && y<=160) || (x>=300 && x<=340 && y>=75 && y<=160);
    if(inArms) return 0.18 + Math.sin(row*0.5+col*0.3)*0.12;
    if(inBody) return 0.12 + Math.sin(row*0.3+col*0.2)*0.1;
    return 0.02;
  }

  function getC3Activation(row, col, rows, cols) {
    var nx = col / cols, ny = row / rows;
    var inTorso = nx >= 0.28 && nx <= 0.72 && ny >= 0.14 && ny <= 0.48;
    var inLeftArm = nx >= 0.05 && nx <= 0.22 && ny >= 0.14 && ny <= 0.42;
    var inRightArm = nx >= 0.78 && nx <= 0.95 && ny >= 0.14 && ny <= 0.42;
    var inHead = nx >= 0.35 && nx <= 0.65 && ny >= 0 && ny <= 0.18;
    if (inRightArm) return 0.5 + Math.sin(row * 1.1 + col * 0.7) * 0.15;
    if (inTorso) return 0.3 + Math.sin(row * 0.9 + col * 0.5) * 0.1;
    if (inLeftArm) return 0.2 + Math.sin(row * 0.8 + col * 0.6) * 0.08;
    if (inHead) return 0.25 + Math.sin(row * 1.2) * 0.1;
    return 0.02;
  }

  function getC4Activation(row, col, rows, cols) {
    var cx = cols / 2, cy = rows * 0.5;
    var colf = col, rowf = row;
    var dist = Math.sqrt(Math.pow((colf - cx) / (cols / 6), 2) + Math.pow((rowf - cy) / (rows / 6), 2));
    var base = Math.max(0, 1 - dist / 2.5) * 0.6;
    var armBoost = (colf > cols * 0.45 && rowf > rows * 0.2 && rowf < rows * 0.55) ? 0.3 : 0;
    return Math.min(1, base + armBoost);
  }

  /* 7×7 reference pattern upsampled to POPW C5 grid (20×15) for canvas visualization */
  function getC5Activation(row, col, rows, cols) {
    var sy = Math.min(6, Math.floor(((row + 0.5) / rows) * 7));
    var sx = Math.min(6, Math.floor(((col + 0.5) / cols) * 7));
    return C5_ACTIVATIONS[sy][sx];
  }

  function drawFeatureMap(canvas, mode, color) {
    if (!canvas) return;
    var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    var W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    var rgb = hexToRgb(color);

    var configs = {
      c1: { cols: 160, rows: 120, fn: getC1Activation },
      c2: { cols: 80, rows: 60, fn: getC2Activation },
      c3: { cols: 40, rows: 30, fn: getC3Activation },
      c4: { cols: 20, rows: 15, fn: getC4Activation },
      c5: { cols: 20, rows: 15, fn: getC5Activation }
    };
    var cfg = configs[mode];
    if (!cfg) return;

    var cellW = W / cfg.cols;
    var cellH = H / cfg.rows;

    /* Always draw faint skeleton under feature activations for spatial grounding */
    var edges = [[5,6],[5,7],[7,9],[6,8],[8,10],[5,11],[6,12],[11,12],[11,13],[13,15],[12,14],[14,16],[0,5],[0,6]];
    ctx.save();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba('+rgb.r+','+rgb.g+','+rgb.b+',0.22)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.15;
    edges.forEach(function(e) {
      var a = JOINTS[e[0]], b = JOINTS[e[1]];
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y); ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y); ctx.stroke();
    });
    JOINTS.forEach(function(j) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(j.x, j.y, j.wrist ? 3.2 : 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba('+rgb.r+','+rgb.g+','+rgb.b+',0.20)';
      ctx.fill();
    });
    ctx.restore();

    for (var row = 0; row < cfg.rows; row++) {
      for (var col = 0; col < cfg.cols; col++) {
        var activation = cfg.fn(row, col, cfg.rows, cfg.cols);
        if (activation > 0.05) {
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba('+rgb.r+','+rgb.g+','+rgb.b+','+(activation * 0.7)+')';
          ctx.fillRect(col*cellW, row*cellH, cellW - 0.5, cellH - 0.5);
        }
      }
    }

    /* Grid lines for all modes; decimate for very dense maps for readability */
    {
      var denseStep = cfg.cols >= 120 ? 8 : (cfg.cols >= 50 ? 4 : (cfg.cols >= 28 ? 2 : 1));
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba('+rgb.r+','+rgb.g+','+rgb.b+',' + (cfg.cols <= 28 ? '0.15' : '0.10') + ')';
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      for (var c = denseStep; c < cfg.cols; c += denseStep) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(c*cellW, 0); ctx.lineTo(c*cellW, H); ctx.stroke();
      }
      for (var r = denseStep; r < cfg.rows; r += denseStep) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, r*cellH); ctx.lineTo(W, r*cellH); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }

    /* C5: label high-activation cells (20×15 POPW stride-32 grid) */
    if (mode === 'c5') {
      ctx.font = 'bold 8px monospace';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      for (var sy = 0; sy < cfg.rows; sy += 2) {
        for (var sx = 0; sx < cfg.cols; sx += 2) {
          var a = getC5Activation(sy, sx, cfg.rows, cfg.cols);
          if (a > 0.6) {
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';
            ctx.fillText(a.toFixed(2), sx * cellW + cellW / 2, sy * cellH + cellH / 2);
          }
        }
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(56,189,248,0.9)';
      ctx.font = '8px monospace';
      ctx.textAlign = 'left';
      ctx.fillText('\u2190 wrist region', 13 * cellW + cellW * 0.5 - 4, 9 * cellH + 12);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(56,189,248,0.92)';
      ctx.font = '9px monospace';
      ctx.fillText('20×15 spatial (640×480 @ stride 32) — C5 [B,2048,20,15] per model.py', 8, H - 8);
    }
  }

  /* ---- FIX 7: FiLM overlay with anatomical body regions ---- */
  function drawFilmOverlay(canvas, frame) {
    if (!canvas) return;
    var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    var W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    var gUser = parseFloat(frame.dataset.gamma || '1', 10);
    var bUser = parseFloat(frame.dataset.beta || '0', 10);
    var tc = getThemeColors();
    var tealRgb = hexToRgb(tc.teal);
    var textRgb = hexToRgb(tc.text);
    var bgRgb = hexToRgb(tc.bg);

    /* Regions aligned to JOINTS (j10 right wrist ~318,152): stronger right-arm emphasis, torso slightly left-biased to avoid washing out the reaching arm */
    var REGIONS = [
      {x:288, y:72,  w:62, h:88,  baseGamma:1.88, label:'R.Arm\n\u03B3='},
      {x:304, y:128, w:36, h:36,  baseGamma:1.92, label:'R.Wrist\n\u03B3='},
      {x:155, y:75,  w:55, h:85,  baseGamma:1.28, label:'L.Arm\n\u03B3='},
      {x:0,   y:0,   w:140,h:H,  baseGamma:0.22, label:'BG\n\u03B3='},
      {x:340, y:0,   w:140,h:H,  baseGamma:0.18, label:'BG\n\u03B3='},
      {x:0,   y:210, w:W,  h:60, baseGamma:0.15, label:'Floor\n\u03B3='},
      {x:168, y:73,  w:105,h:90, baseGamma:0.98, label:'Torso\n\u03B3='},
      {x:140, y:158, w:260,h:60, baseGamma:0.20, label:'Legs\n\u03B3='}
    ];

    REGIONS.forEach(function(r) {
      var g = Math.max(0, Math.min(2.5, r.baseGamma * gUser + bUser));
      var overlayColor;
      if (g > 1.4) {
        var intensity = Math.min(1, (g - 1.0) * 0.4);
        overlayColor = 'rgba('+tealRgb.r+','+tealRgb.g+','+tealRgb.b+',' + intensity + ')';
      } else if (g < 0.4) {
        var darkI = Math.min(0.75, (0.4 - g) * 1.2);
        overlayColor = 'rgba('+bgRgb.r+','+bgRgb.g+','+bgRgb.b+',' + darkI + ')';
      } else {
        overlayColor = 'rgba('+textRgb.r+','+textRgb.g+','+textRgb.b+',0.08)';
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = overlayColor;
      ctx.fillRect(r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h);

      if (g > 1.3 || g < 0.5) {
        ctx.font = 'bold 9px monospace';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillStyle = g > 1.3
          ? 'rgba('+tealRgb.r+','+tealRgb.g+','+tealRgb.b+',0.95)'
          : 'rgba(248,113,133,0.85)';
        var lines = (r.label + g.toFixed(2)).split('\n');
        lines.forEach(function(line, i) {
          ctx.fillText(line, r.x + r.w/2, r.y + r.h/2 - 6 + i*12);
        });
      }
    });
  }

  function drawAnnotations(frame, allJoints) {
    var ann = frame.querySelector('.lf-annotations');
    if (!ann) return;
    var tc = getThemeColors();
    while (ann.firstChild) ann.removeChild(ann.firstChild);
    var list = allJoints ? JOINTS : JOINTS.filter(function(j) { return j.wrist || j.root || j.key; });
    list.forEach(function(joint) {
      var x = joint.x, y = joint.y;
      var lx = x > 240 ? x + 36 : x - 36;
      var ly = y - 28;
      var line = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'line');
      line.setAttribute('x1', x);
      line.setAttribute('y1', y);
      line.setAttribute('x2', lx);
      line.setAttribute('y2', ly);
      line.setAttribute('stroke', joint.root ? '#fbbf24' : (joint.wrist ? '#38bdf8' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'));
      line.setAttribute('stroke-width', '0.9');
      line.setAttribute('stroke-dasharray', '2,2');
      var tw = allJoints ? 102 : 92;
      var th = allJoints ? 20 : 26;
      var tx = x > 240 ? lx - tw : lx - tw;
      var bg = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'rect');
      bg.setAttribute('x', tx);
      bg.setAttribute('y', ly - th);
      bg.setAttribute('width', tw);
      bg.setAttribute('height', th);
      bg.setAttribute('rx', '3');
      bg.setAttribute('fill', tc.bg);
      bg.setAttribute('fill-opacity', '0.88');
      bg.setAttribute('stroke', joint.root ? tc.gold : tc.teal);
      bg.setAttribute('stroke-width', '0.5');
      var t1 = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'text');
      t1.setAttribute('x', tx + tw / 2);
      t1.setAttribute('y', ly - th + (allJoints ? 12 : 10));
      t1.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
      t1.setAttribute('font-size', allJoints ? '6.5' : '7');
      t1.setAttribute('font-family', 'JetBrains Mono, monospace');
      t1.setAttribute('fill', joint.root ? tc.gold : tc.teal);
      var c = joint.coords;
      t1.textContent = joint.id + ' (' + c[0].toFixed(2) + ',' + c[1].toFixed(2) + ',' + c[2].toFixed(2) + ')';
      var g = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'g');
      g.appendChild(line);
      g.appendChild(bg);
      g.appendChild(t1);
      ann.appendChild(g);
    });
  }

  function drawLossAnnotations(frame) {
    var ann = frame.querySelector('.lf-annotations');
    if (!ann) return;
    while (ann.firstChild) ann.removeChild(ann.firstChild);
    var jw = null;
    for (var ji = 0; ji < JOINTS.length; ji++) {
      if (JOINTS[ji].id === 'j10') { jw = JOINTS[ji]; break; }
    }
    if (!jw) jw = { x: 318, y: 152 };
    var gtX = jw.x;
    var gtY = jw.y;
    var predX = jw.x + 4;
    var predY = jw.y;
    var dx = predX - gtX, dy = predY - gtY;
    var err = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy).toFixed(2);

    var gtC = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'circle');
    gtC.setAttribute('cx', gtX);
    gtC.setAttribute('cy', gtY);
    gtC.setAttribute('r', '9');
    gtC.setAttribute('fill', 'none');
    gtC.setAttribute('stroke', '#4ade80');
    gtC.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2');
    gtC.setAttribute('stroke-dasharray', '3,2');
    ann.appendChild(gtC);

    var pr = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'circle');
    pr.setAttribute('cx', predX);
    pr.setAttribute('cy', predY);
    pr.setAttribute('r', '7');
    pr.setAttribute('fill', 'rgba(251,146,60,0.25)');
    pr.setAttribute('stroke', '#fb923c');
    pr.setAttribute('stroke-width', '1.5');
    ann.appendChild(pr);

    var ln = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'line');
    ln.setAttribute('x1', gtX);
    ln.setAttribute('y1', gtY);
    ln.setAttribute('x2', predX);
    ln.setAttribute('y2', predY);
    ln.setAttribute('stroke', '#ef4444');
    ln.setAttribute('stroke-width', '1.5');
    ann.appendChild(ln);

    var lb = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'text');
    lb.setAttribute('x', predX + 14);
    lb.setAttribute('y', predY - 10);
    lb.setAttribute('font-size', '9');
    lb.setAttribute('font-family', 'JetBrains Mono, monospace');
    lb.setAttribute('fill', '#ef4444');
    lb.textContent = '|err|=' + err + 'px';
    ann.appendChild(lb);

    var tGt = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'text');
    tGt.setAttribute('x', gtX - 48);
    tGt.setAttribute('y', gtY + 4);
    tGt.setAttribute('font-size', '8');
    tGt.setAttribute('fill', '#4ade80');
    tGt.setAttribute('font-family', 'JetBrains Mono, monospace');
    tGt.textContent = 'GT (' + Math.round(gtX) + ',' + Math.round(gtY) + ')';
    ann.appendChild(tGt);

    var tPr = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'text');
    tPr.setAttribute('x', predX - 48);
    tPr.setAttribute('y', predY + 22);
    tPr.setAttribute('font-size', '8');
    tPr.setAttribute('fill', '#fb923c');
    tPr.setAttribute('font-family', 'JetBrains Mono, monospace');
    tPr.textContent = 'Pred (' + Math.round(predX) + ',' + Math.round(predY) + ')';
    ann.appendChild(tPr);

    var cap = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'text');
    cap.setAttribute('x', 240);
    cap.setAttribute('y', 256);
    cap.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
    cap.setAttribute('font-size', '7');
    cap.setAttribute('fill', 'rgba(251,146,60,0.92)');
    cap.textContent = 'Pixel L2 gradient ∝ err · Wing uses w=0.05, ε=0.005 (losses.py); |∂L/∂x| = w/(ε+|x|) for |x|<w';
    ann.appendChild(cap);
  }

  function fillFspGrid(withoutEl, withEl) {
    if (!withoutEl || !withEl) return;
    var WGRID = 20, HGRID = 15, N = WGRID * HGRID;
    function cell(val) {
      var d = document.createElement('div');
      d.className = 'fsp-cell';
      d.style.background = 'rgba(56,189,248,' + (0.12 + val * 0.5) + ')';
      return d;
    }
    withoutEl.innerHTML = '';
    withEl.innerHTML = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) {
      withoutEl.appendChild(cell(0.35 + (i % 5) * 0.02));
    }
    for (var j = 0; j < N; j++) {
      var row = Math.floor(j / WGRID), col = j % WGRID;
      var r7 = Math.min(6, Math.floor(row / HGRID * 7));
      var c7 = Math.min(6, Math.floor(col / WGRID * 7));
      var v = 0.28;
      if (r7 >= 2 && r7 <= 4 && c7 >= 3 && c7 <= 5) v = 0.75 + (r7 + c7) * 0.02;
      if (r7 === 4 && c7 === 4) v = 0.95;
      withEl.appendChild(cell(v));
    }
  }

  function updateDescription(wrap, mode) {
    var fid = wrap.querySelector('.living-frame');
    if (!fid) return;
    var id = fid.getAttribute('data-frame-id');
    var el = document.getElementById('lf-desc-' + id);
    if (!el) return;
    if (id === 'frame-problem') {
      if (mode === 'rgb') {
        el.textContent = 'The camera captures this frame. The person is inserting a dowel (illustrative pose in the teaching proxy).';
        return;
      }
      if (mode === 'c5') {
        el.textContent = 'ResNet-50 deepest features — abstract spatial patterns exist, but without pose conditioning the network cannot know which ones are tied to this assembly phase.';
        return;
      }
    }
    if (DESC[mode]) el.textContent = DESC[mode];
  }

  function toggleFsp(wrap, mode, show) {
    var panel = wrap.querySelector('.feature-stage-panel');
    if (!panel) return;
    var showPanel = show && (mode === 'c5' || mode === 'film');
    panel.hidden = !showPanel;
    if (showPanel) {
      var id = panel.id;
      var name = document.getElementById(id + '-name');
      var tensor = document.getElementById(id + '-tensor');
      if (name) name.textContent = mode === 'film' ? 'C5 after FiLM (illustrative)' : 'C5: Semantic Features';
      if (tensor) tensor.textContent = '[2048 × 20 × 15]';
      fillFspGrid(document.getElementById(id + '-without'), document.getElementById(id + '-with'));
    }
  }

  function renderCurrentMode(frame) {
    var mode = frame.dataset.mode || 'rgb';
    var feat = frame.querySelector('.lf-features');
    var film = frame.querySelector('.lf-film-overlay');
    var ann = frame.querySelector('.lf-annotations');
    if (feat) feat.getContext('2d').clearRect(0, 0, feat.width, feat.height);
    if (film) film.getContext('2d').clearRect(0, 0, film.width, film.height);
    if (ann) while (ann.firstChild) ann.removeChild(ann.firstChild);

    var ml = frame.querySelector('.lf-mode-label');
    var tl = frame.querySelector('.lf-tensor-label');
    var al = frame.querySelector('.lf-activity-label');

    if (mode === 'rgb') {
      if (ml) ml.textContent = 'RGB Frame';
      if (tl) tl.textContent = '[B, 3, 480, 640]';
      if (al) al.textContent = '';
    } else if (mode === 'skeleton') {
      if (ml) ml.textContent = 'Pose vector p';
      if (tl) tl.textContent = 'ℝ⁵¹ (17 × 3D + conf)';
      if (al) al.textContent = 'Insert dowel (illustrative)';
      drawAnnotations(frame, true);
    } else if (FEATURE_MAPS[mode]) {
      var fm = FEATURE_MAPS[mode];
      if (ml) ml.textContent = fm.label;
      if (tl) tl.textContent = fm.tensor;
      if (al) al.textContent = '';
      drawFeatureMap(feat, mode, fm.color);
    } else if (mode === 'film') {
      if (ml) ml.textContent = 'FiLM: γ·C5 + β';
      if (tl) tl.textContent = 'γ, β ∈ ℝ²⁰⁴⁸';
      if (al) al.textContent = 'Arm channels amplified';
      drawFeatureMap(feat, 'c5', FEATURE_MAPS.c5.color);
      drawFilmOverlay(film, frame);
    } else if (mode === 'loss') {
      if (ml) ml.textContent = 'Wing loss';
      if (tl) tl.textContent = 'keypoint regression';
      if (al) al.textContent = '';
      drawLossAnnotations(frame);
    }

    var wrap = frame.closest('.living-frame-wrap');
    if (wrap) {
      updateDescription(wrap, mode);
      toggleFsp(wrap, mode, true);
    }

    var hint = document.getElementById('lf-arch-c5-hint');
    if (hint) {
      hint.hidden = !(frame.getAttribute('data-frame-id') === 'frame-arch' && mode === 'c5');
    }
  }

  /* ---- FIX 1: Scope joint queries to frame via data-joint-id ---- */
  function initJointTooltips(frame) {
    var fid = frame.getAttribute('data-frame-id');
    var tooltip = document.getElementById('lf-tooltip-' + fid);
    if (!tooltip) return;
    JOINTS.forEach(function(joint) {
      var el = frame.querySelector('[data-joint-id="' + joint.id + '"]');
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
        var c = joint.coords;
        var html = '<span style="color:#fbbf24">' + joint.id + '</span> <strong>' + joint.name + '</strong><br>' +
          '<span style="color:#38bdf8">x=' + c[0].toFixed(2) + ', y=' + c[1].toFixed(2) + ', z=' + c[2].toFixed(2) + '</span>';
        if (joint.wrist) html += '<br><span style="color:#4ade80">★ Discriminative for assembly</span>';
        if (joint.root) html += '<br><span style="color:#fbbf24">★ Coordinate origin</span>';
        if (joint.key) html += '<br><span style="color:#7dd3fc">★ Shoulder pivot</span>';
        tooltip.innerHTML = html;
        var fr = frame.getBoundingClientRect();
        var jr = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        var tx = jr.left - fr.left + jr.width / 2;
        var ty = jr.top - fr.top - 8;
        tooltip.style.left = Math.min(Math.max(4, tx - 80), fr.width - 200) + 'px';
        tooltip.style.top = Math.max(4, ty - 72) + 'px';
        tooltip.classList.add('visible');
      });
      el.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
        tooltip.classList.remove('visible');
      });
    });
  }

  function initModeButtons(frame) {
    var fid = frame.getAttribute('data-frame-id');
    if (!fid) return;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-frame="' + fid + '"].lf-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
      var modeName = btn.getAttribute('data-mode') || 'unknown';
      if (!btn.getAttribute('aria-label')) {
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Set ' + fid + ' frame to ' + modeName + ' mode');
      }
      btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var mode = btn.getAttribute('data-mode');
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-frame="' + fid + '"].lf-btn').forEach(function(b) {
          b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);
        });
        frame.dataset.mode = mode;
        renderCurrentMode(frame);
      });
    });
  }

  function bindSliders() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.lf-gamma-slider').forEach(function(sl) {
      sl.addEventListener('input', function() {
        var id = sl.getAttribute('data-lf-target');
        var fr = document.querySelector('[data-frame-id="' + id + '"]');
        if (fr) {
          fr.dataset.gamma = sl.value;
          var d = document.querySelector('.lf-gamma-disp[data-lf-target="' + id + '"]');
          if (d) d.textContent = parseFloat(sl.value).toFixed(2);
          fr.dataset.mode = 'film';
          document.querySelectorAll('[data-frame="' + id + '"].lf-btn').forEach(function(b) {
            b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-mode') === 'film');
          });
          renderCurrentMode(fr);
        }
      });
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.lf-beta-slider').forEach(function(sl) {
      sl.addEventListener('input', function() {
        var id = sl.getAttribute('data-lf-target');
        var fr = document.querySelector('[data-frame-id="' + id + '"]');
        if (fr) {
          fr.dataset.beta = sl.value;
          var d = document.querySelector('.lf-beta-disp[data-lf-target="' + id + '"]');
          if (d) d.textContent = parseFloat(sl.value).toFixed(2);
          fr.dataset.mode = 'film';
          document.querySelectorAll('[data-frame="' + id + '"].lf-btn').forEach(function(b) {
            b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-mode') === 'film');
          });
          renderCurrentMode(fr);
        }
      });
    });
  }

  /* ---- FIX 2: Corrected step-to-mode mapping + stepper-change event ---- */
  var STEP_MODES = ['rgb','skeleton','skeleton','film','loss','c5','film','loss','film','film','film'];

  function syncWorkedFrame(step) {
    var frame = document.querySelector('[data-frame-id="frame-worked"]');
    if (!frame) return;
    var mode = STEP_MODES[step] || 'rgb';
    frame.dataset.mode = mode;
    /* Clear annotations on backward navigation */
    var ann = frame.querySelector('.lf-annotations');
    if (ann) while (ann.firstChild) ann.removeChild(ann.firstChild);
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-frame="frame-worked"].lf-btn').forEach(function(b) {
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-mode') === mode);
    });
    renderCurrentMode(frame);
  }

  function patchStepper() {
    if (window.__popwLivingFrameStepperPatched) return;
    var orig = window.showStep;
    if (typeof orig !== 'function') return;
    window.__popwLivingFrameStepperPatched = true;
    window.showStep = function(n) {
      orig(n);
      syncWorkedFrame(n);
      try {
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('stepper-change', { bubbles: true, detail: { step: n } }));
      } catch (e) {}
    };
    syncWorkedFrame(typeof window.currentStep === 'number' ? window.currentStep : 0);
  }

  /* FIX 2: Listen for stepper-change on any frame */
  document.addEventListener('stepper-change', function(e) {
    var frame = document.querySelector('[data-frame-id="frame-worked"]');
    if (!frame) return;
    var mode = STEP_MODES[e.detail.step] || 'rgb';
    frame.dataset.mode = mode;
    renderCurrentMode(frame);
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-frame="frame-worked"].lf-btn').forEach(function(b) {
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-mode') === mode);
    });
    var stepEl = document.getElementById('step' + e.detail.step);
    if (stepEl && typeof window.rerenderMath === 'function') {
      setTimeout(function() { window.rerenderMath(stepEl); }, 0);
    }
  });

  function initAll() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.living-frame').forEach(function(frame) {
      if (frame.dataset.lfBound) {
        renderCurrentMode(frame);
        return;
      }
      frame.dataset.lfBound = '1';
      initJointTooltips(frame);
      initModeButtons(frame);
      renderCurrentMode(frame);
    });
    if (!window.__lfSlidersBound) {
      window.__lfSlidersBound = true;
      bindSliders();
    }
    patchStepper();
  }

  function rerenderAll() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.living-frame').forEach(function(frame) { renderCurrentMode(frame); });
  }

  window.LivingFrame = { initAll: initAll, renderCurrentMode: renderCurrentMode, syncWorkedFrame: syncWorkedFrame, rerenderAll: rerenderAll };

  try {
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function() { rerenderAll(); });
    mo.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-theme'] });
  } catch (e) {}

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initAll);
  } else {
    initAll();
  }
  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    try {
      initAll();
    } catch (e) {}
  });

  (function wireArchDiagram() {
    var wrap = document.querySelector('.arch-flow-wrap');
    if (!wrap) return;
    function setArchMode(mode) {
      if (!mode) return;
      var frame = document.querySelector('[data-frame-id="frame-arch"]');
      if (!frame) return;
      frame.dataset.mode = mode;
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-frame="frame-arch"].lf-btn').forEach(function(b) {
        b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-mode') === mode);
      });
      if (window.LivingFrame && typeof window.LivingFrame.renderCurrentMode === 'function') {
        window.LivingFrame.renderCurrentMode(frame);
      }
      try { frame.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' }); } catch (e) {}
    }
    wrap.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
      var hit = ev.target.closest('.arch-hit[data-arch-mode]');
      if (!hit) return;
      ev.preventDefault();
      setArchMode(hit.getAttribute('data-arch-mode'));
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('keydown', function(ev) {
      if (ev.key !== 'Enter' && ev.key !== ' ') return;
      var hit = ev.target.closest('.arch-hit[data-arch-mode]');
      if (!hit) return;
      ev.preventDefault();
      setArchMode(hit.getAttribute('data-arch-mode'));
    });
  })();
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>
